Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格量化了分类中对抗训练模型的统计与学习理论性质，建立了经验最优部分传输的统计性质，给出了两类中心极限定理及相关的稳定性、样本复杂度和泛化误差集中概率结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练模型在分类中的统计与学习理论性质尚缺乏严格的量化分析，作者希望通过研究经验最优部分传输的统计性质来填补这一空白。

Method: 基于最优势的唯一性（针对各种等价最优传输形式）与经验过程理论论证，证明两类中心极限定理：以经验期望值为中心的CLT，以及带平滑、以总体值为中心的CLT；在二分类情形下，利用最优部分传输与所推导的多边际最优传输公式之间的联系证明最优势的唯一性。

Result: 证明了两类中心极限定理；在二分类情形下证明了最优势的唯一性；作为副产品，得到了对抗训练模型鞍点的稳定性，以及泛化误差的样本复杂度和集中概率。

Conclusion: 该工作为对抗训练模型提供了严格的统计与学习理论刻画，将经验最优部分传输的统计性质与对抗训练的泛化分析联系起来。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is to rigorously quantify the statistical and learning-theoretic properties of adversarial training models for classification. Equivalently, we establish the statistical properties of empirical optimal partial transport. Precisely, first we provide two types of central limit theorems (CLT): CLT centered at the expected empirical value, and CLT centered at the population one with smoothing. These results are based on the uniqueness of optimal potential for various equivalent optimal transport formulations, and the empirical process theory argument. For the binary setting, we indeed prove the uniqueness of optimal potential by leveraging the connection between optimal partial transport and the derived multi-marginal optimal transport formula. As byproducts, we also obtain the stability of a saddle point of the adversarial training model, and the sample complexity and concentration probability of the generalization error.

</details>


### [211] [Universal Observatory Graphs for Distributed Sky Coverage and Artificial Intelligence Based Interplanetary Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22244)
*Mohammed Abdel Razek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出通用观测图（UOG）框架，用加权图建模太阳系L2点自主天文台网络，评估Sky覆盖并求解多目标通信路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向太阳系范围分布式天文观测，需要统一的数学表示来刻画自主观测台网络，并支持覆盖评估与多目标通信路由等协同决策。

Method: 将日-行星L2点的自主观测台建模为加权图节点，边以星际距离、通信时延、传输功率、链路可靠性等物理与运行指标表征；以地球、火星、木星、土星、天王星、海王星六观测台为配置。瞬时天空覆盖用200,000方向Fibonacci球、2,000,000方向固定种子Monte Carlo与确定性球面积分三种方法独立评估；通信路由建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，用表格Q学习求解，奖励平衡节点参与、距离相关可靠性代理与距离、光行时延、距离平方传输功率代理。

Result: 三种覆盖评估方法均给出完整的网络并集覆盖，约0.43%的六观测台完整交集覆盖，约24.96%的平均成对Jaccard相似度；Q学习得到的Earth-Saturn-Uranus-Neptune路径在四跳约束下也是全部41条可行简单路径中折扣回报最高的路径。

Conclusion: 该框架为顺序覆盖评估与多目标路由提供了可复现的基线；时变星历、任务特定可见性、校准链路预算与可扩展图策略等留作未来工作。

Abstract: This research proposes the Universal Observatory Graph (UOG), an AI-driven framework for distributed astronomical observation across the Solar System. The proposed architecture models autonomous observatories located at the Sun planet L2 Lagrange points as nodes in a weighted graph, while communication links are represented as graph edges characterized by multi-objective physical and operational metrics, including interplanetary distance, communication latency, transmission power, and link reliability. The resulting graph provides a unified mathematical representation of a cooperative interplanetary observatory network. This proposal examines a six-observatory Solar System configuration comprising Earth, Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, Uranus and Neptune. Instantaneous sky coverage is evaluated independently using a 200,000 direction Fibonacci sphere, a 2,000,000 direction fixed seed Monte Carlo calculation and deterministic spherical integration. All three methods yield complete network union coverage, approximately 0.43% complete six observatory intersection and approximately 24.96% mean pairwise Jaccard similarity under the adopted pointing model. Communication routing is subsequently formulated as a finite horizon Markov decision process and solved using tabular Q-learning. The reward balances node participation and a distance dependent reliability proxy against distance, light time latency and a distance squared transmission power proxy. The learned Earth-Saturn-Uranus-Neptune route is also the highest discounted return route among all 41 feasible simple paths under the four hop constraint. The framework provides a reproducible baseline for sequential coverage assessment and multi objective routing; time dependent ephemerides, mission specific visibility, calibrated link budgets and scalable graph policies remain future work.

</details>


### [212] [CHART: A Harness-Rotation Curriculum for Harness-Robust Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22247)
*Xinlu Zhang,Ying-Chun Lin,Zhihan Zhang,Besnik Fetahu,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHART（课程式harness轮换训练），通过轮换课程让搜索智能体逐步在多个harness上固化并行搜索行为，解决后训练行为与训练harness纠缠、harness更新即失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体通常只在单一harness下训练，但部署后harness常被更新（如重写系统提示）。由于学到的行为与训练harness纠缠，即使任务不变，harness更新也可能无法激发该行为。作者研究并行搜索这一策略，发现固定harness训练会使行为"harness局部化"，一旦harness改变模型就退回串行搜索。直觉上的harness增广（训练更多harness）并不能解决问题。

Method: 作者首先诊断问题根源：GRPO依赖同一问题下并行与串行rollout之间的奖励差，小的harness池会使该差距过早饱和，而大的harness池又会把每个harness的信号稀释得过薄，使任何harness都无法固化行为。据此提出CHART（课程式Harness轮换训练）：在每个周期性评估中，"毕业"掉已学会预期行为的harness，替换为仍可学习的harness，使奖励差在训练全程保持存在。

Result: 从相同harness池出发，CHART使模型在所有harness上都学会并行搜索，而静态增广最多只在一半harness上成功。行为还能迁移到未见过（held-out）的harness：CHART在89%的held-out轮次上并行化，静态池最多仅5%。进一步迁移到新的QA任务和搜索环境，相比最佳静态池pass@1提升5.6pp。最后，CHART训练的智能体从meta-harness搜索中获益也多于基线。

Conclusion: 通过轮换课程动态管理harness池、维持奖励差，可以解耦行为与特定harness，使并行搜索行为跨harness、跨任务、跨环境稳健泛化，并更好地配合meta-harness搜索。

Abstract: Search agents are usually trained under a single harness. But once an agent is deployed in a real application, its harness is frequently updated (e.g., a rewritten system prompt) to fit production needs. This exposes a fragility of post-trained agents: because a learned behavior is entangled with its training harness, even a harness update that leaves the task unchanged can fail to elicit the behavior. We train a search agent to perform parallel search, a popular strategy for improving both search efficiency and performance. We find that training under a fixed harness makes the behavior harness-local, overfit to that harness's surface form: when the harness changes, the model falls back to serial search. An intuitive fix is harness augmentation, but simply training on more harnesses does not resolve the problem. GRPO learns from the reward gap between parallel and serial rollouts of the same question: a small harness pool saturates that gap early, while a large pool dilutes the per-harness signal too thinly for any harness to consolidate. We therefore propose Curriculum HArness Rotation Training (CHART), a rotating curriculum that lets a search agent gradually consolidate parallel search across harnesses. At each periodic evaluation, CHART "graduates" the harnesses whose expected behavior is learned and replaces them with still-learnable ones, keeping the reward gap alive throughout training. Starting from the same harness pool, CHART makes the model learn parallel search on all harnesses, whereas static augmentation succeeds on at most half of them. The behavior also carries to held-out harnesses: CHART parallelizes on 89% of held-out turns, against at most 5% for the static pools. It further transfers to a new QA task and search environment, improving pass@1 by 5.6pp over the best static pool. Finally, CHART-trained agents benefit more from meta-harness search than baselines.

</details>


### [213] [Predictors and Orchestrators: Parsimonious Machine Learning within an Agentic AI Harness for Multi-Horizon Karst Aquifer Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22251)
*Pramod Lekhak,Chetan Sharma,Hakan Başağaoğlu,F. Paul Bertetti,Debaditya Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于美国得克萨斯州Edwards含水层约79年水文气候观测数据，建立并评估了一个面向部署的岩溶含水层动态预测框架，用于提前1–12周预测泉流量与地下水位。研究比较了XGBoost、极端随机树、LSTM、CNN和Transformer五类模型，发现XGBoost在各预测时段表现最稳定，R²分别不低于0.97、0.96和0.94，并在干旱阈值判定上具有较高准确率；深度模型短期表现可竞争，但随预测期延长性能下降。研究将验证后的模型嵌入五智能体运维架构，实现数据获取、模型分配、预测、阈值监测、前瞻验证、文献检索和报告生成的自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 岩溶含水层动态预测具有挑战性，因为补给响应呈非线性、事件驱动，并受高度非均质流动路径控制。研究动机在于构建一个可直接部署、可迁移的预测框架，同时兼顾模型选择的简洁性、评估的泄漏感知与多时段设计、对业务干旱阈值的决策相关性，以及可审计的自动化流程。

Method: 研究使用Edwards含水层约79年水文气候观测数据，在统一时间评估设计下比较五类模型：极端梯度提升、极端随机树、长短期记忆网络、卷积神经网络和Transformer。预测任务为提前1–12周的泉流量和地下水位预测，并按1–4周、5–8周、9–12周划分预测时段。评价指标包括决定系数、Kling-Gupta效率、均方根误差，以及与业务干旱阈值的吻合程度。验证后的模型被嵌入一个五智能体运维架构，用于自动化数据获取、模型分配、确定性预测、阈值监测、前瞻验证、文献检索和报告。

Result: 极端梯度提升在各时段中表现最可靠：1–4周、5–8周和9–12周预测时段的R²分别至少为0.97、0.96和0.94；在所有预测时段中，对前三个干旱阶段的关键阶段吻合度均超过90%。深度模型在短期预测中具有竞争力，但随着预测期延长性能逐步下降，并在较长提前期出现个别失败。作者将这种差异归因于树模型的分区方式与低维、轴对齐的水文气候预测变量相匹配，同时神经模型倾向于平滑不规则极端事件。

Conclusion: 该研究贡献在于提出一个可迁移框架，将简约模型选择、泄漏感知的多时段评估、与决策相关的阈值技能和可审计的智能体自动化结合起来，可用于岩溶含水层泉流量与地下水位的业务化预测。

Abstract: Forecasting karst aquifer dynamics is difficult because recharge responses are nonlinear, event-driven, and governed by strongly heterogeneous flow paths. This study develops and evaluates a deployment-aware framework for 1-12-week-ahead prediction of spring discharge and groundwater level using approximately 79 years of hydroclimatic observations from the Edwards Aquifer, Texas. Five model families were compared under a common temporal evaluation design: extreme gradient boosting, extremely randomized trees, long short-term memory, convolutional neural networks, and Transformers. Predictions were evaluated using coefficient of determination, Kling-Gupta efficiency, root-mean-square error, and agreement with operational drought thresholds. Extreme gradient boosting was consistently most reliable, with R2 at least 0.97, 0.96, and 0.94 across 1-4-, 5-8-, and 9-12-week horizons, respectively, and greater than 90% critical-stage agreement at the first three drought stages across all horizons. Deep models were competitive at short horizons but degraded progressively and exhibited isolated failures at longer lead times. We attribute this contrast to an alignment between tree partitioning and low-dimensional, axis-aligned hydroclimatic predictors, together with the tendency of neural models to smooth irregular extremes. The validated models were embedded in a five-agent operational architecture that automates data acquisition, model assignment, deterministic prediction, threshold monitoring, prospective verification, literature retrieval, and reporting. The contribution is therefore a transferable framework joining parsimonious model selection, leakage-aware multi-horizon evaluation, decision-relevant threshold skill, and auditable agentic automation.

</details>


### [214] [CALM: A Calibrated LLM Choice Network Framework for Activity-Based Traveler Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22252)
*Yezhou Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALM 是一个可复现的混合出行模拟框架，将可选的大语言模型活动规划器与校准随机选择、记忆习惯、可行性检查及确定性回放整合，通过闭环出行日循环和实证基线评估各生成模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多为出行模式分类器或仅生成日志的工具，缺乏闭环的出行日模拟与可复现的模块级评估，难以在干预情境下检验生成式规划器的表现。

Method: 基于2024年纽约市城市交通调查数据，按受访者划分训练集（78,487次出行）与留出集（32,204次出行），采用仅训练集的替代特定常数校准；结合可选LLM活动规划器、校准随机选择、共享网络反馈、记忆与习惯、类型化可行性检查及确定性离线回放；并通过匹配的LLM消融、冻结提示-响应对和天气、延误、票价与停车等受控干预阶梯进行评估。

Result: 仅训练集校准将留出集出行模式的平均Jensen-Shannon散度从0.15599降至0.00394（十个随机种子）；LLM消融量化了聚合拟合、时间拟合、行为持续性与可行性之间的权衡；受控干预实验显示出一致且可解释的响应。

Conclusion: CALM 提供了整合与评估生成式规划器于出行者模拟的可复现协议，涵盖个体不相交校准、匹配模块消融、受控压力测试与端到端可追溯性。

Abstract: We present CALM, a reproducible hybrid framework that integrates an optional large language model (LLM) activity planner with calibrated stochastic choice, shared network feedback, memory and habit, typed feasibility checks, and deterministic offline replay. Unlike trip-mode classifiers or diary-only generators, CALM executes a closed traveler-day loop and evaluates each generative module against an empirical, reproducible baseline. On the 2024 New York City Citywide Mobility Survey (CMS), 110,691 seven-mode trips are split by respondent into 78,487 training and 32,204 holdout trips. Training-only alternative-specific constant calibration reduces mean holdout mode Jensen-Shannon divergence from 0.15599 to 0.00394 across ten seeds. A matched live-LLM ablation then quantifies trade-offs among aggregate fit, temporal fit, behavioral persistence, and feasibility, while frozen prompt-response pairs support deterministic replay of downstream simulation. Controlled weather, delay, fare, and parking ladders further demonstrate consistent and interpretable responses under intervention. CALM contributes a reproducible protocol for integrating and evaluating generative planners in traveler simulation through person-disjoint calibration, matched module ablation, controlled stress testing, and end-to-end traceability.

</details>


### [215] [CAMFT: Conflict-Aware Mergeable Fine-Tuning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22253)
*Jingang Zhou,Haiyang Guo,Yuan Ma,Han Zhu,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAMFT，将可合并性视为微调阶段塑造的属性，通过引导各任务更新跨任务冲突更低的稀疏坐标，实现高效且可合并的任务适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法多聚焦于对独立微调模型的后期处理，忽视了训练阶段对跨任务兼容性的影响；微调后再解决参数冲突本质上并非最优。

Method: CAMFT（Conflict-Aware Mergeable Fine-Tuning）：在微调过程中引导每个任务更新稀疏坐标，选择跨任务冲突较低的参数位置进行更新，使任务更新训练高效且更具下游合并兼容性。

Result: 大量实验表明，在多任务合并场景下，CAMFT优于标准微调基线方法。

Conclusion: CAMFT将可合并性视为微调阶段塑造的属性而非事后处理的问题，为多任务能力整合提供了更优的训练范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a promising paradigm for integrating multiple task-specific capabilities into a single large language model. However, existing methods predominantly focus on post-hoc processing of independently fine-tuned models, overlooking how the training phase itself impacts cross-task compatibility. Resolving parameter conflicts after fine-tuning is inherently sub-optimal. To address this, we propose CAMFT, a Conflict-Aware Mergeable Fine-Tuning method that makes task adaptation both efficient and mergeaware. CAMFT treats mergeability as a property shaped during fine-tuning, rather than only a problem to be solved after fine-tuning. By guiding each task to update sparse coordinates with lower cross-task conflict, CAMFT produces task updates that are efficient to train and more compatible for downstream model merging. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CAMFT outperforms standard finetuning baselines in multi-task merging scenarios. Codes are available at https://github.com/gyanchow/CAMFT-LLM.

</details>


### [216] [Teacher Should Think Ahead: Adaptive Continuations for Reliable On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22254)
*Jingang Zhou,Yuyi Zhou,Haiyang Guo,Xukai Wang,Shuai Feng,Sirui Gao,Jian Xu,Qingpei Guo,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了在线策略蒸馏中"教师不确定性收缩(TUC)"现象，并提出自适应延续的AC-OPD方法以提升蒸馏可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏(OPD)中学生沿自身生成轨迹接受教师密集的token级监督，但当学生前缀不完整或质量较低时，教师监督可能不可靠，因此需要理解并解决这一问题。

Method: 作者识别出"教师不确定性收缩(TUC)"现象，即教师从学生生成前缀继续时预测不确定性会降低。他们通过教师分支梯度的方差-偏差分解理论刻画了这一权衡：不确定性收缩降低方差，而师生路径分歧增加偏差，从而倾向于有限的延续。据此提出自适应延续在线策略蒸馏(AC-OPD)，在学生轨迹的informative状态上增加教师延续，并自适应选择其有效监督视野。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上、跨不同模型规模的实验表明，AC-OPD持续优于标准OPD。受控延续和匹配预算分析进一步验证了自适应延续设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应教师延续是可靠在线策略蒸馏的有效原则。代码将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a promising approach for transferring knowledge between language models, where a student receives dense token-level supervision along its own generated trajectories. However, teacher supervision can be unreliable when conditioned on incomplete or low-quality student prefixes. We identify Teacher Uncertainty Contraction (TUC), a systematic phenomenon whereby the teacher's predictive uncertainty decreases as it continues from a student-generated prefix. We theoretically characterize this trade-off through a variance-bias decomposition of teacher-branch gradients, showing that uncertainty contraction reduces variance while teacher-student path divergence increases bias, thereby favoring a finite continuation. Guided by this insight, we propose Adaptive-Continuations On-Policy Distillation (AC-OPD), which augments informative states along student rollouts with teacher continuations and adaptively selects their effective supervision horizons. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation across model scales demonstrate that AC-OPD consistently improves over standard OPD. Controlled-continuations and matched-budget analyses further validate the adaptive-continuations design, highlighting adaptive teacher continuations as an effective principle for reliable on-policy distillation.The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [217] [Strategy Accumulation and Guided Execution for Automated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22257)
*Haoran Zhao,Wei Du,Dingwen Yang,Jixuan Huang,Junlin Shang,Lingyong Fang,Ya Guo,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（Strategy Accumulation and Guided Execution），一个两阶段框架，使自动微调搜索具备累积性。第一阶段用多智能体流水线进行蒙特卡洛树搜索并蒸馏出跨任务经验仓库；第二阶段检索并选择适用经验指导新任务训练。在九个未见任务上，单轮执行的平均相对提升从3.2%提高到15.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产生任务专用大模型需通过实验发现有效训练策略，自动微调系统虽降低了人工成本，但它们是无状态的：每次搜索结束后会丢弃已发现的策略、数据洞察和超参发现，导致每个新任务都要从冷启动重新进行昂贵搜索。因此需要让自动微调搜索具备累积性。

Method: SAGE为两阶段框架。第一阶段：多智能体流水线执行基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的探索，并行的蒸馏智能体提取任务特定的探索记录与带置信度评分的跨任务洞察，共同构成结构化经验仓库。第二阶段：从该仓库检索相关经验，并选择适用的部分来指导新任务的训练。

Result: 在涵盖单类别与跨类别设置的九个未见任务上评估。单轮执行中，SAGE的累积经验将相对基线的平均相对提升从3.2%提高到15.6%，相比不带该经验的原流水线提升了12.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，持久化的策略经验能够为未见任务的自动微调提供有效指导。

Abstract: Producing task-specific large language models requires discovering effective training strategies through experimentation. Automated fine-tuning systems have made this experimentation feasible with far less manual effort. However, these systems are stateless: each search discards its discovered strategies, dataset insights, and hyperparameter findings once it ends. Every new task must then repeat this costly search from a cold start. To address this, we propose Strategy Accumulation and Guided Execution (SAGE), a two-stage framework that makes automated fine-tuning search cumulative. In the first stage, a multi-agent pipeline performs Monte Carlo Tree Search-based exploration. A parallel Distillation Agent extracts task-specific exploration records and confidence-scored cross-task insights, which together constitute a structured experience repository. In the second stage, SAGE retrieves relevant experience from this repository and selects what applies to guide training on the new task. We evaluate SAGE on nine unseen tasks spanning both single- and cross-category settings. In single-round execution, SAGE's accumulated experience raises the average relative improvement over baseline from 3.2% to 15.6%, a 12.4-percentage-point gain over the same pipeline without it. These results show that persistent strategy experience provides effective guidance for automated fine-tuning on unseen tasks.

</details>


### [218] [RS-Claw-Evolution: Environment-Feedback-Driven Evolution for Lightweight Remote Sensing Agents in Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22258)
*Kai Ouyang,Dongyang Hou,Liangtian Liu,Zeyuan Wang,Ziyu Li,Chengfu Liu,Zichao Tang,Xuezhi Cui,Shengwu Ouyang,Wentao Yang,Hanwen Yu,Haifeng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RS-Claw-Evolution框架，通过环境反馈驱动的三阶段进化，显著提升轻量级遥感智能体的多步交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于紧凑语言模型的轻量级遥感智能体在长程多步交互任务中面临三大挑战：长程状态丢失、环境反馈利用低效、优化信号稀疏，导致其性能难以满足自动地理空间分析需求。

Method: 环境反馈驱动的三阶段渐进式框架：1）交互进化——用可执行代码控制观测、维护中间状态、减少上下文冗余；2）经验进化——结合失败感知轨迹生成与错误轮次掩码，从失败-恢复经验中学习而不模仿错误动作；3）决策进化——采用多维环境奖励与轮次级优势保护的强化学习，优化工具使用行为并改进长序列信用分配。

Result: 在Earth-Bench上，优化后的Qwen3-4B智能体在自主规划模式下达到65.9%准确率，优于未训练的Qwen3-32B基线（43.8%）和DeepSeek-V3.1（60.8%），并接近GPT-5（71.6%）。

Conclusion: 从环境反馈中学习能有效提升轻量级遥感智能体，缩小其与大型模型在长程遥感任务上的性能差距。

Abstract: Large language model-driven remote sensing (RS) agents offer a promising approach to automating geospatial analysis. However, lightweight RS agents based on compact language models struggle with multi-step interactive tasks due to loss of long-horizon states, inefficient environmental feedback utilization, and sparse optimization signals. We propose RS-Claw-Evolution, an environment-feedback-driven framework that progressively improves lightweight agents through three stages. Interaction evolution uses executable code to control observations, maintain intermediate states, and reduce context redundancy. Experience evolution combines failure-aware trajectory generation with error-turn masking to learn from informative failure-recovery experiences without imitating faulty actions. Decision evolution uses reinforcement learning with multi-dimensional environment rewards and turn-level advantage protection to optimize tool-use behaviors and improve credit assignment in long sequences. On Earth-Bench, the optimized Qwen3-4B-based agent achieves 65.9% accuracy in Autonomous Planning mode, outperforming the untrained Qwen3-32B baseline (43.8%) and DeepSeek-V3.1 (60.8%), while approaching GPT-5 (71.6%). These results demonstrate that learning from environmental feedback can improve lightweight agents and narrow their performance gap with larger models in long-horizon RS tasks.

</details>


### [219] [Resist, Update, Reject: Preference Optimization Installs a Prior-Dependent Reliability Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22359)
*Sen Yang,Yuen-Hei Yeung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 反谄媚目标的偏好优化在源可靠性维度上不可识别，仅产生单一的"顺从刻度"；通过阈值基准与平衡覆盖的偏好优化，可让模型安装依赖先验的可靠性开关，实现基于被陈述可靠性的翻转行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流反谄媚工作优化的目标在源可靠性上不可识别：偏好标签不依赖于源是否真实可靠，因此任意标量混合只能沿单一顺从刻度移动，无法区分仅在被陈述可靠性上不同的同模板证词，导致"固执↔轻信"前沿成为目标函数的属性而非模型属性。作者希望让可靠性变得可识别。

Method: 提出阈值基准：源陈述与正确答案相反的答案并声明其可靠性 r，正确动作是当且仅当 r 超过模型先验强度 p 时翻转。在 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 上以平衡覆盖做偏好优化，用三个种子验证；并设置三项对照（不匹配变体、第二偏好优化器 IPO、监督模仿）定位成因，另做确认性测试、边界界定与跨模型迁移。

Result: 三个种子下阈值 r* 随先验单调上升，决策准确率达 0.84，翻转曲线单调（Spearman 0.56）；策略泛化到未见可靠性值与留出记号，并优先遵循被陈述的可靠性而非角色声望。对照中不匹配变体同样安装开关（0.80），IPO 亦然（0.86），监督模仿则不能（0.50），说明成因是可靠性标注覆盖上的偏好优化，而非配对、损失或模仿。

Conclusion: 该"固执↔轻信"前沿是经验现象而非定理。确认性测试在新抽取样本上复现了开关，并诚实界定其边界：它依据证词中陈述的可靠性，而非单独审计的记录；同时可迁移到 Llama-3.1-8B。

Abstract: An aligned model asked to hold its answer against a manipulative source must still update on a reliable one and reject an unreliable one: resistance, reliable-update, and unreliable-source rejection are one three-way contract, not three independent behaviors. We show the objective most anti-sycophancy work optimizes is non-identifying with respect to source reliability: because no preference label depends on whether a source is actually reliable, any scalar mixture of the arms traces a single deference dial, and no point separates two same-template testimonies differing only in stated reliability. This fixation$\leftrightarrow$gullibility frontier is a property of the objective, not any model. We make reliability identifiable through data: a threshold benchmark where a source asserts the opposite answer while stating its reliability $r$, and the correct action is to flip iff $r$ exceeds the model's prior strength $p$. Preference optimization over balanced coverage installs a prior-dependent reliability switch: across three seeds on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct the threshold $r^\star$ rises monotonically with the prior, decision accuracy reaches $0.84$ with a monotone flip curve (Spearman $0.56$), and the policy generalizes to unseen reliability values and a held-out notation, following stated reliability over role prestige. Three controls localize the cause: an unmatched variant installs the switch equally ($0.80$), a second preference optimizer (IPO) installs it just as well ($0.86$), whereas supervised imitation does not ($0.50$), so the cause is preference optimization over reliability-labeled coverage, not pairing, loss, or imitation. A confirmatory battery replicates the switch on a fresh test draw, bounds it honestly (it keys on reliability stated in the testimony, not a separately audited record), and transfers it to Llama-3.1-8B. The frontier is empirical, not a theorem.

</details>


### [220] [Contrastive Siamese Representation Learning for Predictive Maintenance of Electrical Submersible Pumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22360)
*Seshu K. Damarla,Xiuli Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合类别不平衡感知的故障诊断框架，采用孪生对比表示学习与先验校正KNN分类，用于电潜泵（ESP）故障诊断，并通过留一泵体策略验证其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电潜泵在海上石油生产中至关重要，意外故障会带来重大运营与经济损失。然而，由于运行工况非线性、类别不平衡以及泵体间差异，ESP系统的精确预测性维护仍具挑战性。

Method: 1）从振动域指标与工程化谐波关系中提取故障检测判别特征；2）使用类别平衡的对比样本对训练孪生神经网络，构建使同类故障聚集、异类故障分离的嵌入空间；3）在分类阶段采用先验校正的距离加权KNN以进一步缓解类别不平衡。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在真实工业条件下能提供稳健且一致的故障分类性能，支持其在可靠预测性维护与智能ESP系统监测中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效应对非线性工况、类别不平衡和泵体差异问题，在未见过ESP单元上表现出良好泛化能力，具备实际应用前景。

Abstract: Electrical submersible pumps (ESPs) are essential in offshore oil production, where unexpected failures can result in significant operational and financial losses. Accurate predictive maintenance for ESP systems remains challenging due to nonlinear operating conditions, class imbalance, and variability among pump units. To address these issues, this study presents a fault diagnosis framework that incorporates class imbalance awareness by employing Siamese contrastive representation learning and prior-corrected k-nearest neighbor (KNN) classification. The method first extracts discriminative features relevant to fault detection from vibration-domain indicators and engineered harmonic relationships. A Siamese neural network is trained with class-balanced contrastive pairs to construct an embedding space that clusters samples of the same fault type and separates different fault classes. To further mitigate class imbalance during classification, a prior-corrected distance-weighted KNN is applied. The framework is validated using a Leave-One-ESP-Out (LOEO) strategy to evaluate generalization to previously unseen ESP units. Experimental results indicate that the proposed framework delivers robust and consistent fault classification performance under realistic industrial conditions, supporting its potential for reliable predictive maintenance and intelligent ESP system monitoring.

</details>


### [221] [Common Cause, Not Cross-Attention: Blocking Visual Shortcuts in Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22361)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控因果研究揭示音视频联合生成模型依赖视觉捷径（从外观而非因果事件预测声音），并证明仅靠共享隐变量无法修复，必须对冗余变量实施干预以实现反事实不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频联合生成器的训练数据中，事件的外观与声音高度（且常为虚假）相关，导致模型可能学到虚假捷径而非真正的因果生成机制，需要系统性地研究这一失败模式及其成因。

Method: 构建音视频结构因果模型（SCM），使音频在构造上与视频的冗余外观独立；对比直接读取（cross-attention/共享隐变量）与共享共因潜变量（瓶颈、无监督共享/私有分解、共享先验模型）等架构；并通过反事实不变性理论证明因果预测器的可识别性；在特征向量SCM、程序化像素视频、真实图像+频谱音频（含预训练骨干）、移动数字及条件生成器上验证。

Result: 让音频直接读取视频的模型学到视觉捷径，在测试时破坏外观-事件相关性后崩溃，合成错误事件的声音；共享共因潜变量的常见补救措施无效，仍抓取外观代理并失败；只有在冗余变量上实施干预才能阻断捷径；在真实预训练视频到音频生成器上，输入干预测试显示模型对声音无关编辑（重着色或灰度化视频）远非不变，会显著改变生成的声音。

Conclusion: 音视频生成中的虚假相关导致视觉捷径，共享隐变量等常规方法无法解决；在给定SCM与干预假设下，反事实不变性是识别因果预测器的充要条件，需对冗余变量进行干预以阻断捷径。

Abstract: Joint audio--video generators are trained on data in which what an event looks like and what it sounds like are strongly, often spuriously, correlated: a particular material, texture, or object appearance co-occurs with a particular sound. This paper is a controlled causal study of the resulting failure mode. Building an AV structural causal model in which the audio is, by construction, independent of the video's nuisance appearance, we show that models which let audio read video directly-through cross-attention or a shared latent-learn a visual shortcut: they predict sound from appearance rather than from the causal event, and collapse when the appearance-event correlation is broken at test time, literally synthesizing the wrong event's sound. Crucially, the popular remedy of routing both modalities through a shared common-cause latent does not fix this: a bottleneck, an unsupervised shared/private factorization, and a faithful shared-prior model all grab the appearance proxy and fail like the direct model. Blocking the shortcut instead requires an intervention on the nuisance. Under the stated SCM and intervention assumptions we prove that counterfactual invariance is necessary and sufficient to identify the causal predictor, and we verify the mechanism across a feature-vector SCM, procedural pixel video, real images with spectrogram audio and a pretrained backbone, moving real digits, and a conditional generator. On a \emph{real, pretrained} video-to-audio generator, an input-intervention test shows the model is far from invariant to sound-irrelevant edits (recolouring or graying a video substantially changes the sound it generates).

</details>


### [222] [Complex-valued Phase-Coherent Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22415)
*Leona Hioki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 复值Transformer引入缩放余弦注意力（L2归一化Q/K，得分保持一阶尺度），构成相位相干Transformer（PCT）家族，在长程记忆、位置检索、层次推理、频域分类和物理复信号任务上匹配或超过最强实值基线，并首次以真正复值网络解决Path-X。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复值Transformer沿用了基于原始复内积的softmax注意力，但在非原生复值领域中这种标准形式几乎停留在随机水平，且此前没有任何复值注意力被证明能纠正这一问题，因此需要找出正确的匹配形式。

Method: 提出将匹配形式改为缩放余弦得分：对查询和键做L2归一化，使得分读作其余弦相似度并忽略其幅值，同时把该得分保持在一阶尺度。在四种诊断任务、两种门控下训练同一模型；对照实验包括不做归一化以及归一化但尺度过小的变体。

Result: 不做归一化时，在两种门控下ListOps与Needle均停留在随机水平，另两项任务远低于基线；归一化但尺度过小同样失败。PCT在五类任务上匹配或超过最强实值基线；深度20内无退化；参数在61倍范围内损失呈对数线性下降。家族成员（复值筛选结合相位相干递归）以91.6%可训练参数为复值（S4为38.2%）首次解决Path-X。

Conclusion: 缩放余弦匹配与一阶尺度是复值注意力有效性的关键；PCT展现出复值神经网络此前未见过的泛化迹象。

Abstract: Complex-valued Transformers have inherited softmax attention over the raw complex inner product. Outside natively complex domains this standard form stays near chance, and no complex attention had been shown to correct it. We show that the match must be a scaled cosine score: L2-normalise queries and keys, so the score reads their cosine similarity and ignores their magnitudes, and hold that score at order-one scale. With this the same models train on four diagnostic tasks under two different gates; without the normalisation they stay at chance on ListOps and Needle under both gates and fall far below on the other two, and a normalised score placed at too small a scale fails as well. The resulting family of phase-coherent Transformers (\PCT) matches or exceeds the strongest real-valued baseline across long-range memory, positional retrieval, hierarchical reasoning, frequency-domain classification and physical complex signals; it shows no degradation up to depth 20; and its loss decreases log-linearly over a 61-fold range of parameters. A member of the family, complex screening combined with a phase-coherent recurrence, is the first genuinely complex-valued neural network to solve Path-X, with 91.6% of its trainable parameters complex-valued against 38.2% for S4. We record these as signs of generalisation not previously seen in complex-valued neural networks.

</details>


### [223] [Connected Content Retriever: Dense Graph Edge Features Powering Pre-Ranking at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22441)
*Akhilesh Gupta,Sudarshan Srinivasa Ramanujam,Chirag Bhanuprasad Mehta,Reshma Asharaf Beena,Dhritiman Das,Birjodh Singh Tiwana,Bhargavkumar Kanubhai Patel,Mack Lee,Renyi Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LinkedIn 提出 CC Retriever 预排序系统，用 GPU 上的深度排序模型对网络内容候选进行低延迟打分，实现模型参数 50 倍扩展，线上内容停留时长提升 2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 LinkedIn Feed 等大规模推荐系统中，成员网络（连接与关注）产生的内容占曝光和互动的 70% 以上，因此预排序层必须选出最优的数百个候选交给排序层。由于一/二度网络互动信号（如 stranger viral）导致候选索引超过十亿，需在 120ms p99 延迟预算内对约数万条活动打分，传统方案难以支撑。

Method: 提出 Connected Content Retriever（CC Retriever）预排序系统，在 GPU 上以完整深度排序模型对候选打分。核心是一个排序搜索（sorted-search）GPU 原语，将稠密的图亲和力特征（viewer 到 author）与文档级特征在运行时于 GPU 上做连接，耗时 5-10ms；并利用 LinkedIn 经济图谱的特征集与相应模型架构，构建可扩展的线上打分栈。

Result: GPU 服务化打分使排序模型参数规模扩展 50 倍，线上实验显示 LinkedIn Feed 内容停留时长提升 +2.5%，显著高于 LinkedIn Feed 实验的常规增益；GPU 上的特征连接在 5-10ms 内完成。

Conclusion: CC Retriever 证明在严格延迟预算下用 GPU 部署完整深度排序模型进行预排序是可行且高效的，能显著提升模型容量与线上互动指标；论文重点描述了所依赖的经济图谱特征、模型架构及支撑该栈的线上系统。

Abstract: In large-scale recommendation systems like the LinkedIn Feed, content generated by a member's network (connections and follows) makes up over 70% of impressions and engagement. It is therefore essential that the pre-ranking layer forwards the best possible few hundred candidates to the ranking layer. LinkedIn's professional knowledge graph carries engagement signals across both the first degree network (connections and follows) and the second-degree network: posts that a 1st-degree connection reacted to, commented on or reshared but did not author (a.k.a. stranger viral). Due to this fan out, the resulting candidate index exceeds one billion; selection of activities from the viewer's network narrows it down to roughly tens of thousands of activities that must be scored within a 120 ms p99 latency budget. We present Connected Content Retriever (CC Retriever), a pre-ranking system that scores these candidates with a full deep ranking model on GPUs at low latency. At its core is a sorted-search GPU primitive that joins dense graph affinity features (viewer to author) with document level features stored on the GPU at runtime in 5-10 ms. The shift to GPU served scoring enabled a 50x scale up of the ranking model's parameters and delivered a +2.5% lift in content time spent on the LinkedIn Feed in online experiments, significantly higher than the typical gains observed in LinkedIn Feed experiments. In this work, we describe the feature set we leverage from LinkedIn's economic graph and the model architecture used for scoring, with a particular emphasis on the online system that scales the stack.

</details>


### [224] [Efficient Mixture-of-Experts with Speculative Decoding via Expert Coactivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22471)
*Kumari Nishu,Han-Byul Kim,Santosh Chilkunda,Maxwell Horton,Arnav Kundu,Mohammad Samragh,Lauren Hannah,Mohammad Sekhavat,Nikhil Bhendawade,Manuel Ciosici,Iman Mirzadeh,Keivan Alizadeh Vahid,David Harrison,Irina Belousova,Mehrdad Farajtabar,Minsik Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MoE路由设计对与推测解码（SD）结合时推理速度的影响，发现高专家共激活能显著提速，并通过组合多种训练策略提升吞吐21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE与推测解码（SD）结合可加速推理，但SD依赖并行验证多个token，验证token越多，从DRAM传输到NPU的专家就越多，内存传输成本上升，而内存传输通常是推理瓶颈，因此结合两者面临挑战。

Method: 研究MoE路由器设计对SD速度的影响，分析不同路由设计选择对专家共激活与运行时的影响，并在十亿参数级Transformer模型上评估。具体考察全局负载均衡损失、共享专家、一致性损失以及自回归专家选择机制等训练策略的组合效果。

Result: 发现高专家共激活的路由器能显著加快运行时并缓解更多验证token带来的成本；组合全局负载均衡损失、共享专家、一致性损失与自回归专家选择机制可显著增强专家共激活，最终模型相比MoE基线吞吐提升21%，且精度与基线MoE相当。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计路由器训练策略以提升专家共激活，可有效缓解MoE结合SD时的内存传输瓶颈，在不损失精度的前提下显著提升推理吞吐。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are increasingly deployed alongside Speculative Decoding (SD) to accelerate inference, but combining the two is challenging. SD improves the inference speed of dense models by verifying groups of tokens in parallel. However, the inference speedup for SD with MoEs depends heavily on the number of tokens being verified. Using more verification tokens results in more experts being transferred from DRAM to the Neural Processing Unit (NPU), which increases the memory transfer cost. This negatively impacts model runtime, as memory transfer is typically the bottleneck in inference. In this work, we investigate the impact of MoE router design during training on the speed of MoEs with SD. We find that routers with high degrees of expert coactivation result in much faster runtimes, mitigating the impact of using more verification tokens. Motivated by this observation, we assess the impact of various router design choices on expert coactivation and runtime using billion-parameter transformer models. We find that combining a global load-balancing loss, shared experts, a consistency loss, and an autoregressive expert selection mechanism during training results in significantly stronger expert coactivation. This increased coactivation translates into higher overall runtime throughput: our exploration yields a model that improves throughput by 21% over MoE baselines, while maintaining on-par accuracy with the baseline MoE.

</details>


### [225] [COREM: Cosine-Relation Momentum Reshaping with Stateful Writeback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22487)
*Yan Wang,Xiaochuan Wang,Yuxiang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出单位关系变换抽象及COREM方法，利用矩阵型优化器状态中的关系结构改进优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵型优化器状态可能含有关联结构，而逐元素独立处理会忽略这些关系，因此研究能否利用状态内部关系提升优化效果。

Method: 提出单位关系变换抽象，并实例化为COREM（余弦关系动量重塑，带状态写回）。COREM将动量状态划分为更新单元，计算单元间余弦关系，据此重塑动量并将变换后的状态写回优化器，使重塑动量影响当前及未来优化动态。

Result: 在CIFAR-10（MLP）和enwik8（Transformer）上评估。相比Muon，COREM早期步效率较低，但中后期提升更强，CIFAR-10最终验证性能更优，enwik8最终性能相当。谱诊断显示COREM提高熵有效秩、降低主导模态奇异能量集中，同时保持各向异性谱。方形矩阵更新下，COREM变换FLOPs约为Muon（5次Newton-Schulz迭代）的13.3%。

Conclusion: 利用矩阵优化器状态中的关系结构可带来中后期优化收益，并在保持谱多样性的同时显著降低变换计算成本。

Abstract: Matrix-valued optimizer states may contain relational structure that is not captured by treating their entries independently. We study whether relations within matrix-valued optimizer states can be exploited to improve optimization. To this end, we introduce a unit-relation-transform abstraction and instantiate it as COREM, a Cosine-Relation Momentum Reshaping method with stateful writeback. COREM partitions the momentum state into update units, computes cosine relations among them, and uses these relations to reshape the momentum before writing the transformed state back to the optimizer. This stateful mechanism allows the reshaped momentum to affect not only the current update but also future optimization dynamics. We evaluate COREM on CIFAR-10 with an MLP and on enwik8 with a Transformer. Compared with Muon, COREM shows lower early-stage step efficiency but stronger improvement in the mid-to-late stages of training, achieving better final validation performance on CIFAR-10 and comparable final performance on enwik8. Spectral diagnostics on enwik8 show that COREM consistently increases entropy effective rank and reduces the concentration of singular energy in dominant modes, while preserving an anisotropic spectrum. For square matrix updates, COREM requires approximately 13.3% of the transformation FLOPs of Muon with five Newton-Schulz iterations.

</details>


### [226] [EmbeddGAN: A Novel GAN Framework Using an Embedding Network and Gini Distance Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22508)
*MaTais Caldwell,Yixin Chen,Xin Dang,Charles Walter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EmbeddGAN，一种基于依赖性目标的对抗训练框架，用嵌入网络替代传统判别器，通过Gini距离相关系数（gCor）度量真实/生成样本与其标签的统计依赖性，实现更稳定的GAN训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GAN缺乏关于收敛性和学习过程有效性的形式化保证，实践中导致训练不稳定、模式崩溃以及对超参数敏感。作者希望从依赖性度量角度重构对抗训练目标，以获得更稳定的训练动态。

Method: EmbeddGAN不依赖对样本分类为真/假的判别器，而是引入一个嵌入网络学习表示，使样本与其真实/虚假标签之间的统计依赖性最大化，同时训练生成器最小化该依赖性。该目标用Gini距离相关系数（gCor）实现，gCor为零当且仅当嵌入与真实/虚假标签统计独立。嵌入网络将真实和生成数据投影到共享低维空间，通过成对距离直接度量分布差异。采用极小极大训练策略：嵌入网络最大化gCor（最大化依赖性），生成器最小化gCor（最小化依赖性）。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CelebA数据集上的实验表明，EmbeddGAN相对于已有基线取得了有竞争力的性能，同时在所评估数据集上展现出显著稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: 通过用基于gCor的依赖性目标替代传统判别器分类目标，EmbeddGAN在保持生成质量竞争力的同时改善了训练稳定性，为GAN的对抗训练提供了一种新的依赖性度量视角。

Abstract: Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) have demonstrated strong performance in generating high-quality synthetic data. However, they are limited by no formal guarantees regarding convergence and the effectiveness of the learning process. In practice, this leads to training instability, mode collapse, and sensitivity to hyperparameters. To address this, we propose EmbeddGAN, a novel adversarial training framework based on a dependence-based objective. Instead of relying on a discriminator that classifies samples as real or fake, EmbeddGAN introduces an embedding network that learns a representation in which statistical dependence between samples and their real/fake labels is maximized, while the generator is trained to minimize this dependence. This objective is implemented using the Gini distance correlation (gCor), which equals zero if and only if the embeddings are statistically independent of the real/fake label. Minimizing this objective therefore encourages real and generated samples to become statistically indistinguishable in the learned embedding space. The embedding network projects both real and generated data into a shared low-dimensional space, where distributional discrepancies can be measured directly through pairwise distances. We adopt a minimax training strategy: the embedding network maximizes the Gini distance correlation (maximizing dependence), while the generator minimizes it (minimizing dependence). Experiments on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CelebA datasets demonstrate that EmbeddGAN achieves competitive performance relative to established baselines while exhibiting notably stable training dynamics on the evaluated datasets.

</details>


### [227] [The Ups and Downs of Backprop Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22554)
*Giuseppe Chindemi,Benjamin F. Grewe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"权重算子"（weight operators）以实现可复用、可组合的功能组件，解决BP下的权重纠缠问题；Vector Networks为其一个实现，通过两阶段学习将算子选择与局部误差驱动更新耦合，为"功能参数可识别性"提供检验基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BP虽推动深度学习成功，但未规定参数应如何组织以实现功能组件的复用与选择性适应。识别与运动预测等功能可能依赖重叠参数集，导致难以独立隔离或修改，作者称之为"权重纠缠"。现有架构（非线性门控、注意力、MoE路由）虽做动态选择，却不能保证同一功能组件跨样本始终绑定到可识别的参数集。

Method: 提出权重算子：参数化模块，实现可复用功能组件，并可在推理时组合成每个样本所需的函数。学习分两阶段：先推断所需的算子组合，再仅更新被选中算子的参数集。Vector Networks（VNs）作为一种实现，将算子选择与每层内局部误差驱动更新耦合。

Result: VNs表明学习到的算子可在训练中未出现的组合中被复用，且更新仍限于被选中的参数集。这为检验功能参数可识别性提供了基础，即算子在学习过程中是否始终与同一功能组件保持绑定。

Conclusion: 作者主张功能参数可识别性可作为一项组织原则，用于系统化复用与重组已学函数、同时只适应需要改变的组件。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) has driven the remarkable success of modern deep learning by enabling large hierarchical networks to learn complex functions end-to-end. Yet it does not by itself determine how parameters should be organized so that functional components can be reused and adapted selectively. For example, object recognition and motion prediction may depend on overlapping parameter sets, making them difficult to isolate or modify independently. We call this condition weight entanglement. Modern architectures dynamically select which parts of a network process each sample: nonlinearities gate units, attention selects interactions, and Mixture-of-Experts architectures route inputs to modules. Yet such selection does not ensure that the same functional component remains linked to an identifiable parameter set across samples. We propose weight operators: parameterized modules that implement reusable functional components and can be composed at inference to form the function required by each sample. Learning proceeds in two stages: the model first infers the required operator composition, then updates only the selected operators' parameter sets. Vector Networks (VNs) provide one implementation. They couple operator selection to local error-driven updates within each layer and show that learned operators can be reused in combinations absent from training while updates remain restricted to the selected parameter sets. This provides a basis for testing functional parameter identifiability: whether an operator remains linked to the same functional component during learning. We argue that functional parameter identifiability may provide an organizing principle for models that systematically reuse and recombine learned functions while adapting only the components that need to change.

</details>


### [228] [Benchmarking Hybrid Deep Learning Architectures for Predictive Maintenance in Industry 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22583)
*Zhengyang,Gu,Joseph E. Hernandez,Thomas Cook,John Burtenshaw,Sean Scott,Chris Couch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文评估六种深度学习架构在700多次实验中的表现，发现Transformer在噪声工厂数据中易过拟合噪声，而LSTM+Transformer混合模型兼具精度与稳定性，是更可靠的预测性维护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业4.0预测性维护模型常出现"实验室表现完美、真实工厂数据失效"的可靠性落差，需要考察主流架构在含噪数据下的鲁棒性。

Method: 针对RNN（逐步处理序列）与Transformer（整体关注序列）两类主流方法，开展超过700次实验，比较六种深度学习架构在含噪数据上的预测表现，并测试LSTM与Transformer结合的混合模型。

Result: Transformer在稳定、缓慢变化的流程中表现优异，但对混沌数据过度反应，易将传感器噪声误判为有效信号；LSTM+Transformer混合模型作为噪声过滤器，平滑数据波动，让Transformer专注整体趋势，不仅提升准确率，还显著比复杂模型更一致、更可靠。

Conclusion: 在真实工厂噪声数据下，混合LSTM-Transformer架构比单纯Transformer或RNN更稳健可靠，可作为提升预测性维护可靠性的有效方案。

Abstract: Predictive maintenance in Industry 4.0 refers to using data from sensors, machines, and production systems to estimate when equipment is likely to fail, so maintenance can be planned before a breakdown occurs [1]. However, a model that predicts maintenance may work perfectly in the lab but fail unexpectedly when applied to real factory data [2]. To solve this "reliability" gap, we evaluated six deep learning architectures across more than 700 experimental runs. We focused on the two dominant approaches in the field: Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs), which process data step-by-step, like reading a sentence [3], and Transformers, a recent dominant approach, which look at the entire sequence at once to spot important connections [4]. We examined whether Transformers still outperform recurrent neural networks (RNNs) when the data includes noise [5]. We found that while Transformers excelled at tracking stable, slow-moving processes, they tend to overreact to chaotic data, mistakenly taking sensor noise for meaningful signals [6]. We also found that the hybrid method that combines a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) layer with a Transformer layer is more resilient to noisy data from factory shops [7]. Functioning as a noise filter, the LSTM smooths out data volatility, allowing the Transformer to focus on the bigger picture without being distracted [8]. The hybrid model did not just improve accuracy; it proved to be significantly more consistent than complex models, delivering reliable predictions regardless of how chaotic the underlying system became.

</details>


### [229] [Augmenting PID Control with Deep Reinforcement Learning: A Hybrid Approach to the Industrial Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22584)
*Zhengyang,Gu,Joseph E. Hernandez,John Burtenshaw,Sean Scott,Thomas Cook,Chris Couch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种混合PID-RL控制器：先设计多目标奖励函数使DRL可行，再用TD3智能体在工业基准(IB)中发现"Gain"与"Shift"的最优非直觉参数，并将其输入调优后的PID控制器，从而兼得DRL的Performance与Efficiency优势以及经典控制器的Reliability。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工业过程日益复杂，传统PID控制器难以应对非线性、多输入动态。作者希望验证深度强化学习(DRL)在此类复杂环境中的优势，但DRL最终策略常不稳定，因此需要一种既利用DRL自主发现能力、又保证可靠性的方法。

Method: 以工业基准(IB)为测试床，该仿真包含高维状态空间、延迟效应和相互冲突的多目标等工业挑战。作者设计多目标奖励函数使DRL可用，并采用双延迟深度确定性(TD3)智能体作为"发现工具"，搜索IB中'Gain'与'Shift'参数的最优、非直觉设定，再将发现的参数馈入一个简单的、已调优的PID控制器，构成混合PID-RL控制器。

Result: 混合模型成功同时具备三项特性：达到最优DRL智能体相当的Performance与Efficiency，同时保持经典控制器的Reliability。测试也显示DRL的核心权衡——最终策略常不稳定，但其独特优势在于能在简单控制器失效的多维空间中发现最优、非直觉的策略。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种实用方法学：用DRL去增强(augment)而非替代(replace)可信赖的工业控制系统，为复杂工业场景下的控制器设计提供了可落地路径。

Abstract: As industrial processes grow in complexity, traditional Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controllers are often insufficient for handling their non-linear, multi-input dynamics. We propose using advanced Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) to prove its advantages in these complex environments. To do this, we rely on the Industrial Benchmark (IB). The IB is a realistic simulation that tests DRL algorithms against the key challenges of industrial applications: high-dimensional state spaces, delayed effects, and conflicting multi-criterial objectives. This testbed highlights DRL's core trade-off: while its final policies can often be unstable, its unique strength is the ability to autonomously discover optimal, non-obvious policies in multi-dimensional spaces where simple controllers fail. In this paper, we propose a novel hybrid PID-RL controller that leverages DRL's discovery capability while ensuring Reliability. After developing a multi-objective reward function to make DRL viable, we use a twin-delayed deep deterministic (TD3) agent as a discovery tool to find the optimal, non-obvious settings for the IB's 'Gain' and 'Shift' parameters. By feeding these discovered parameters to a simple, tuned PID controller, our hybrid model successfully combines all three characteristics: it achieves the optimal Performance and Efficiency of the best DRL agent with the Reliability of a classical controller. This work demonstrates a practical methodology for using DRL to augment, rather than replace, trusted industrial control systems.

</details>


### [230] [TWIG: A Time-Causal Wavelet Operator for Autoregressive Forecasting on Irregular Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22585)
*Subashree Venkatasubramanian,David A. Barajas-Solano,Chuyang Liu,Daniel M. Tartakovsky,Dipankar Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWIG是面向静态不规则图的图原生神经常算子，通过时间因果多尺度特征与图小波算子块实现稳定自回归代理建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则图上的动力场自回归预测需要兼顾因果性与多尺度时间记忆，现有非时间因果基线（如Graph WNO）在闭环预测中稳定性不足，因此需要一种图原生的、构造上即因果的神经算子。

Method: TWIG将每个节点历史转化为因果多尺度时间特征，分离近期变化与逐步变慢的记忆分量；再通过带门控逐点通道混合的图小波算子块传播特征。架构构造上即为因果，专为闭环预测设计，预测结果可递归作为未来输入。

Result: 在区域扩散、三维地下水文和气动流三个不规则域预测任务上评估，图规模400至5233节点，模型容量约7万至1000万参数。TWIG在地下水文和区域扩散基准上取得最低的整体rollout误差，在1000万参数气动流基准上排名第二（仅次于GPS Transformer），并在三种设置中均持续优于对应的非时间因果Graph WNO基线。

Conclusion: TWIG为不规则图上动力场的稳定自回归预测提供了一种有效且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: We introduce TWIG (Time-Causal Wavelet Operator for Irregular Graphs), a graph-native neural operator for autoregressive surrogate modeling on static irregular graphs. TWIG transforms each node history into causal multiscale temporal features that separate recent variation from progressively slower memory components, then propagates these features through graph-wavelet operator blocks with gated pointwise channel mixing. The architecture is causal by construction and designed for closed-loop forecasting, where predictions are recursively reused as future inputs. We evaluate TWIG on three irregular-domain forecasting problems spanning regional diffusion, three-dimensional subsurface hydrology, and aerodynamic flow, with graphs ranging from 400 to 5,233 nodes and model capacities from approximately 70k to 10M parameters. TWIG achieves the lowest aggregate rollout errors on the subsurface-hydrology and regional-diffusion benchmarks and ranks second on the 10M-parameter aerodynamic-flow benchmark, behind the GPS Transformer. Across all three settings, TWIG consistently outperforms the corresponding non-time-causal Graph WNO baseline. These results demonstrate that TWIG provides an effective and scalable approach to stable autoregressive forecasting of dynamical fields on irregular graphs.

</details>


### [231] [User-Level Handover Decision Making Based on Machine Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22593)
*João Lima,Alvaro Medeiros,Eduardo Aguiar,Vicente Angelo de Sousa Junior,Tarciana Guerra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文比较了多种机器学习与模糊逻辑方法在恶劣传播条件下用户级切换决策中的应用，发现SVM和MLP最适合分类最佳切换目标，LightGBM在下载时间估计中误差最小且处理时间短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在建筑物、覆盖空洞和阴影效应等恶劣传播条件的场景中，用户级切换决策面临挑战，需要比较不同方法以寻找适用于分类和回归任务的解决方案。

Method: 基于ns-3网络仿真平台开展仿真，比较了经典机器学习方法（KNN、SVM、神经网络）、先进模糊逻辑系统以及提升机（boosting）方法在切换决策分类和下载时间回归任务中的表现。

Result: SVM和MLP最适合最佳切换目标的分类，模糊系统SOFL性能相近但处理时间更低；在下载时间估计中，LightGBM误差最小且处理时间短，即使在困难传播场景下依然如此。

Conclusion: SVM/MLP适用于切换目标分类，SOFL可作为低时延替代方案，LightGBM适合下载时间估计，为恶劣传播条件下的切换决策方法选择提供了依据。

Abstract: This letter covers a broad comparison of methods for classification and regression applications for a user-level handover decision making in scenarios with adverse propagation conditions involving buildings, coverage holes, and shadowing effects. The simulation campaigns are based on network simulator ns-3. The comparison encompasses classical machine learning approaches, such as KNN, SVM, and neural networks, but also state-of-the-art fuzzy logic systems and latter boosting machines. The results indicate that SVM and MLP are the most suitable for the classification of the best handover target, although fuzzy system SOFL can perform similarly with lower processing time. Additionally, for the download time estimation, LightGBM provides the smallest error with short processing time, even in hard propagation scenarios.

</details>


### [232] [Concurrency-Aware Process Model Forecasting with Causal Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22614)
*Yongbo Yu,Jari Peeperkorn,Johannes De Smedt,Jochen De Weerdt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将过程模型预测(PMF)从直接跟随图扩展到因果网，通过预测关系与绑定计数的时间序列并结合AND/XOR语义重建未来过程模型，并提出考虑部分轨迹的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PMF方法直接预测直接跟随图，而直接跟随图无法显式表示并发行为，因此需要一种能够刻画并发语义的预测方法。

Method: 预测关系与绑定计数的时间序列，并用这些预测结果重建具有AND/XOR语义的未来过程模型；同时引入考虑部分轨迹的评估协议，并构建符合性检查所需的工作流网。

Result: 在四个事件日志上，所预测模型达到的符合度接近从对应未来窗口观测中重新挖掘的模型，并优于静态发现基线；静态基线在结构稳定的日志上保持高精确度，但在其余三个日志上精确度损失显著。过滤低频绑定可改善多数符合度指标，但也会移除大量并发行为。

Conclusion: 将PMF扩展到因果网是可行且有效的，能更好地捕捉并发并取得接近未来观测重挖模型的符合度，但低频绑定过滤在提升符合度的同时会损失并发行为的刻画能力。

Abstract: Process model forecasting (PMF) aims to predict the process model that will characterize a future period, thereby providing a process-level view of how behavior is expected to evolve. Existing PMF methods, however, forecast directly-follows graphs, which cannot explicitly represent concurrency. We extend PMF to causal nets by forecasting time series of relation and binding counts and using these forecasts to reconstruct future process models with AND/XOR semantics. To evaluate the resulting models, we introduce a protocol that accounts for partial traces and constructs the workflow nets required for conformance checking. Experiments on four event logs show that the forecasted models achieve conformance levels close to those of models re-mined from observations in the corresponding future windows. They also outperform static discovery baselines, which retain high precision on the structurally stable log but exhibit substantial precision losses on the other three logs. Filtering infrequent bindings improves most conformance metrics, although it also removes much of the concurrent behavior captured by the models.

</details>


### [233] [Classification with Abstention Under Class-Conditional Error Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22632)
*Mohammadreza M. Kalan,Yuyang Deng,Sanaz Hamidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究带弃权的二分类问题：在分别约束两类条件误差的前提下最小化弃权率，给出无分布假设下的极小极大超额弃权风险率，并提出可计算的替代损失框架与有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际分类任务中，两类错误代价可能不同且常需分别设限，此时允许模型弃权（拒判）比强制分类更合适。已有工作多关注总体风险或单一约束，缺少在分别类条件误差约束下、关于弃权风险的最优学习率刻画，也缺少适配神经网络等模型的可计算方法。

Method: 1) 在分别的类条件误差约束下形式化最小化弃权的二分类问题；2) 以假设类复杂度与样本量刻画超额弃权风险的无分布极小极大速率（忽略对数因子）；3) 引入替代损失（surrogate loss）表述并推导超额替代歧义风险的有限样本保证；4) 将学习任务建模为约束优化问题，并在凸设定下分析其计算复杂度。

Result: 得到超额弃权风险的无分布极小极大最优速率（至对数因子）；给出替代损失下的有限样本泛化界；刻画了相应约束优化问题在凸情形下的计算复杂度；在多个数据集上验证方法并与该问题的一个竞争方法进行性能比较。

Conclusion: 该工作为分别误差约束下的弃权学习提供了理论最优性与可计算性两方面的支撑，并通过实验验证了方法的有效性，为神经网络等模型的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We study binary classification with abstention under separate class-conditional error constraints, with the objective of minimizing abstention while keeping both errors below prescribed thresholds. We characterize the distribution-free minimax rate of excess abstention risk, up to logarithmic factors, in terms of the complexity of the hypothesis class and the sample size. To make the framework amenable to computation with models such as neural networks, we introduce surrogate-loss formulations and derive finite-sample guarantees for excess surrogate ambiguity risk. We formulate the resulting learning task as a constrained optimization problem and characterize its computational complexity in the convex setting. Finally, we evaluate our approach on various datasets and compare its performance with a competing method for this problem.

</details>


### [234] [Monotone-Constrained Diffusion Models for Long-Horizon Production Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22643)
*Temesgen Mikael Abraha,Yves Lucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Physics-SIMS-TS 条件扩散预测模型，用于油气产量长期预测，通过负向引导、递减曲线约束与等渗投影保证单调性，在 3.5 万余口井上取得较高精度与校准区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭序列最初少量观测外推长时程本质上是不适定问题，同一段短历史可对应多条轨迹。油气产量预测中，约前五分之一生产期做出的预测会影响开发与弃井决策，且可用预测必须呈单调递减。

Method: 提出 Physics-SIMS-TS：条件扩散预测器，结合针对合成伪影的负向引导（negative guidance）、递减曲线约束、采样过程中的等渗投影（isotonic projection）、空间训练增强，以及集成随机采样器以给出完整预测分布；并给出骨干网络的可逆实例归一化（reversible instance normalization）变体。

Result: 在三个辖区、超过 35,000 口井、统一空间与验证冻结协议下，Physics-SIMS-TS 是所比较扩散预测器中最准确的，与集成 Transformer 预测器相当但不优于后者；其预测构造上单调，MSE 代价至多 0.5%；轨迹集成在每个辖区拟合一个离散因子后得到校准区间；在六个标准基准上，其归一化变体是领先的扩散基线。同时量化了四个影响排序的协议选择。

Conclusion: 物理约束与等渗投影能以极小精度代价换取预测单调性，扩散集成可实现良好校准；但扩散方法未超越集成 Transformer，且评测排序对协议选择敏感，需谨慎比较。代码与评估产物已公开。

Abstract: Forecasting a long horizon from only the first observations of a sequence is ill-posed: many trajectories are consistent with the same short history. We study this problem in oil and gas production forecasting, where forecasts made after roughly the first fifth of a well's producing life drive development and abandonment decisions, and where a usable forecast must describe a monotone decline. We present Physics-SIMS-TS, a conditional diffusion forecaster that combines negative guidance against synthetic artifacts, decline-curve constraints and an isotonic projection applied during sampling, spatial training augmentation, and an ensembled stochastic sampler yielding a full predictive distribution. Across three jurisdictions and more than 35,000 wells, under a shared-space, validation-frozen protocol, Physics-SIMS-TS is the most accurate diffusion forecaster in the comparison and is competitive with, but not superior to, ensembled transformer forecasters. Its forecasts are monotone by construction at a cost of at most 0.5% in mean squared error, and its trajectory ensemble yields calibrated intervals after one dispersion factor is fitted per jurisdiction. On six standard benchmarks a reversible-instance-normalization variant of the backbone is the leading diffusion baseline. We also quantify four protocol choices on which the measured ranking depends. Code and evaluation artifacts are released.

</details>


### [235] [Multi-Armed Bernoulli Bandits via Minimax Single-Arm Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22690)
*Huikang Liu,Zhengchao Wang,Daniel Kuhn,Wolfram Wiesemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单臂老虎机（SAB）问题极小极大解的有限时域伯努利多臂老虎机指数策略，利用累积续采概率构造指数，并得到 minimax 最优阶的后悔界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时域伯努利多臂老虎机需要具有理论保证且实用的策略。作者希望从单臂问题的极小极大最优停止解出发，得到自然的指数排序机制，从而构造具有无分布后悔保证的策略。

Method: 将每个单臂问题建模为在未知伯努利臂与已知奖励之间的选择，证明其最坏情况后悔最小化可写成精确的半无限线性规划。由停止策略导出比较臂的直觉：对已知奖励续采的门槛越高，未知臂越有前途。据此构造基于累积续采概率的指数，并加入单调调整与奖励缺口上限。通过将指数误差与单臂停止策略的后悔关联，得到后悔界；并用伯努利随机化推广至[0,1]奖励，提供有限网格实现及近似损失量化。

Result: 得到与 K 臂、时域 T 相关的无分布后悔界 4.45√(KT)+10.75K，与文献中的 minimax 最优阶一致；在数值实验中，该 SAB 指数策略在所有测试的臂数和时域下最坏情况后悔均优于各基准策略，并在双臂情形下与基于网格的 MAB minimax 策略接近。

Conclusion: 该工作以单臂极小极大停止解为核心，建立了理论最优阶且实验表现优异的有限时域伯努利多臂老虎机指数策略，并可推广至一般[0,1]奖励与有限网格实现。

Abstract: We develop an index policy for finite-horizon Bernoulli multi-armed bandits from minimax solutions to single-arm bandit (SAB) problems. Each SAB problem involves choosing between an unknown Bernoulli arm and a known reward. We show that minimizing worst-case regret of SAB problems over all non-anticipative policies admits an exact semi-infinite linear programming formulation. The resulting stopping policies offer a natural way to compare arms: the higher the known reward against which a policy continues sampling, the more promising the unknown arm. We turn this intuition into indices based on cumulative continuation probabilities, with a monotone adjustment and a reward-shortfall cap. By relating index errors to the regret of single-arm stopping policies, we establish a distribution-free regret bound of $4.45\sqrt{KT}+10.75K$ for $K$ arms and horizon $T$. This bound matches the minimax-optimal regret order established in the literature. The guarantee extends to rewards supported on $[0,1]$ through Bernoulli randomization. We also provide a finite-grid implementation with quantified approximation loss. In numerical experiments, the SAB-based index policy achieves lower worst-case regret than every tested benchmark policy across all evaluated numbers of arms and horizons, while closely matching the grid-based MAB minimax policy in the two-arm setting.

</details>


### [236] [Autonomous Model Lifecycle Management for Digital Twin-Based Manufacturing Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22701)
*Zhengyang,Gu,Thomas Cook,Fredaljohn Rohrbaugh,Joseph E. Hernandez,Chris Couch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于汽车制造自主模型生命周期管理的闭环信息物理系统（CPS），自2023年部署，通过数字孪生物理模型与深度强化学习控制策略配对、多候选竞争择优及Conductor编排器实现自主再训练与PID回退，在保证安全与操作员信任的前提下显著提升工艺稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业AI系统需在原材料波动、环境变化和设备老化带来的持续分布偏移下自主适应，同时满足严格的安全保障与操作员信任要求，因为模型失效可能导致物理损坏。现有系统难以在自主更新模型与人工信任、安全约束之间取得平衡。

Method: 构建闭环CPS进行自主模型生命周期管理，管理产品专用模型对：作为数字孪生的序列到序列物理模型（LPP）与针对其训练的深度强化学习控制策略（LCP）。每次再训练周期中，多个跨架构族与RL算法的模型变体竞争，仅最优候选晋级。Conductor编排器自主管理全厂模型库存，具备依赖感知再训练与PID回退机制。LCP综合评分嵌入操作员信任门控，惩罚偏离既定实践的策略。

Result: 若缺乏操作员信任门控，23%的策略虽通过精度阈值却被操作员拒绝；在多家工厂中，LCP控制的过程相较未控制基线实现28-45%的工艺稳定性提升，且零安全事故。

Conclusion: 该闭环CPS验证了以人为中心智能原则在工业AI生命周期管理中的有效性，将自主模型更新、安全约束与操作员信任相结合，可在实际制造环境中实现稳定、无事故的部署。

Abstract: Manufacturing AI systems must autonomously adapt to continuous distributional shift from raw-material variability, ambient changes, and equipment aging, under strict safeguard and operator-trust requirements where model failures risk physical damage. This paper presents a closed-loop Cyber-Physical System (CPS) for autonomous model lifecycle management in automotive manufacturing, deployed since 2023. The system manages product-specialized model pairs: a sequence-to-sequence physics model (LPP) serving as a digital twin, and a deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) control policy (LCP) trained against it. Per retraining cycle, multiple model variants spanning architecture families and RL algorithms compete; only the best-scoring candidate advances. A Conductor orchestrator autonomously manages plant-wide model inventories with dependency-aware retraining and Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) fallback. Reflecting the principle of Human-Centric Intelligence, the LCP composite score embeds an operator-trust gate penalizing policies deviating from established practice; without it, 23% of policies are rejected by operators despite passing accuracy thresholds. Across multiple facilities, LCP-controlled processes achieve process stability improvements of 28-45% over uncontrolled baselines with zero safety incidents.

</details>


### [237] [D-IMPL: A Diffusion-based Solver for Parameterized BBOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22752)
*Yang Hu,Na Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散模型的通用求解器 D-IMPL，用于参数化黑盒优化（BBO），通过学习“最小化策略”将求解问题转化为生成建模任务，并在理论上给出 O(log(1/δ)) 的样本复杂度保证，同时在多类约束与非约束任务上进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化中优化器只能查询目标函数值，传统方法在每个实例上推理时往往仍需大量额外计算，且难以刻画非凸目标的多模态特性。作者希望借助扩散模型强大的复杂分布学习能力，摊销不同 BBO 实例间的计算成本，并高效覆盖潜在的多模态景观。

Method: 1) 引入“最小化策略”作为新的解概念，定义在解空间上的采样分布，使其集中于每个实例的最小值点集附近；2) 将其构造为兼容的生成建模任务；3) 提出 D-IMPL（Diffusion-based Iterative Minimization Policy Learning），用扩散模型学习该最小化策略，使其密度正比于目标函数负值的指数，从而摊销不同实例的计算成本；4) 给出样本复杂度理论分析，证明 δ-近似最小化策略可在 O(log(1/δ)) 次迭代内有效学习。

Result: 理论上建立了样本复杂度保证：δ-近似最小化策略可在 O(log(1/δ)) 次迭代内有效学习；实验上在一系列约束与非约束 BBO 任务上进行了广泛评估，验证了 D-IMPL 的性能。

Conclusion: D-IMPL 表明扩散模型可有效用于参数化黑盒优化，通过学习最小化策略将求解过程转化为生成建模，既降低了推理阶段的额外计算成本，又能捕捉非凸目标的多模态结构，并具备理论保证与实证支持。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated strong power in generative modeling tasks across multiple domains, exhibiting a remarkable capability of learning complex distributions from samples. In this paper, we leverage such capability to design an efficient universal diffusion-based solver for parameterized black-box optimizations (BBO), where the optimizer has only black-box access to queries of the objective function at the learning stage, yet is able to reduce the additional computational cost at the inference stage for each BBO instance while also capturing the potential multi-modal landscape of non-convex objectives. To cast our formulation as a compatible generative modeling task, we introduce the notion of minimization policy as a new solution concept, which defines a sampling distribution over the solutions that should concentrate around the minimizer set for each BBO instance. We then propose Diffusion-based Iterative Minimization Policy Learning (D-IMPL), a practical generative-model-based solver for solving parameterized BBOs that employs diffusion models to learn a minimization policy, whose density is proportional to the exponential of the negated objective values, thereby amortizing the computational costs across different BBO instances. Furthermore, we demonstrate the performance of our D-IMPL algorithm by establishing a sample complexity guarantee showing that a $δ$-approximate minimization policy can be effectively learned within $O(\log(1/δ))$ iterations, and by extensive empirical evaluations over a range of constrained and unconstrained BBO tasks.

</details>


### [238] [Look Before You Steer: Geometry Predicts SAE Feature Steerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22782)
*Muhammad Khan,Shlok Channawar,Akshaj Gurugubelli,Girish Gupta,Aditya Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现SAE特征的可操纵性（steerability）部分可由解码器空间的几何性质预测，该性质可在干预前从SAE权重矩阵计算得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导SAE特征目前需要逐特征调节系数并进行干预扫描，成本高昂。作者希望探究是否存在无需前向传播、可预先计算的SAE自身属性，来预测哪些特征引导成本低、哪些成本高。

Method: 作者提出用解码器空间几何（邻居密度、与邻近解码方向的最高余弦相似度）来刻画特征可操纵性，这些指标均可从SAE权重矩阵在干预前算出，并与固定行为效果所需的引导强度做相关性分析。实验覆盖两个Gemma-2规模（2B、9B）、两种SAE宽度（16K、65K），并在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上跨架构验证，另在Qwen3-8B（BatchTopK SAE）上考察边界条件。

Result: 几何指标能部分预测特征引导成本，相关系数ρ最高达-0.546（p<10^-6），AUROC为0.610-0.822；在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上ρ=-0.266（n=300）。在Qwen3-8B的BatchTopK SAE上，几何只能预测特征是否可引导，而不能预测响应特征间的连续排序。信号在深层比例深度处减弱，因为引导成本超出干预预算。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步证据，表明干预前几何信息可部分指导系数选择，为部署前筛选可控特征提供了可行路径，并揭示了与SAE训练机制及层深相关的边界条件。

Abstract: Steering with SAE features requires per-feature coefficient tuning, which currently demands intervention sweeps. We ask whether properties of the SAE itself, computable before any forward pass, predict which features will be cheap or expensive to steer. We show that variation in SAE feature steerability is partially predicted by decoder-space geometry: neighbor density and maximum cosine similarity to nearby decoder directions, both computable from the SAE weight matrix before any intervention, rank features by how much steering they require for a fixed behavioral effect ($ρ$ up to $-0.546$, $p < 10^{-6}$, AUROC 0.610-0.822 across conditions; the signal is rank-based, consistent with grid discreteness). This geometry-steerability relationship replicates across two Gemma-2 model scales (2B and 9B), two SAE widths (16K and 65K), and is detectable cross-architecturally on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct ($ρ= -0.266$, $n = 300$). On Qwen3-8B with BatchTopK SAEs, geometry predicts whether a feature is steerable at all but not the continuous ordering among responsive features, revealing a boundary condition tied to SAE training regime. The signal weakens at deep proportional layer depth in both models, where the cost of steering exceeds our intervention budget, a consistent depth boundary. These results provide preliminary evidence that pre-steering geometry can partially inform coefficient selection, offering a path toward screening features for controllability before deployment.

</details>


### [239] [Improved Private Sparse Covariance Estimation with Multiscale Threshold Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22783)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究均值零、子高斯分布、协方差支撑未知且每行至多 k 个非零项的算子范数差分隐私协方差估计，提出多尺度随机阈值算法，将隐私相关样本复杂度从 Õ(k^{3/2}√d/(αε)) 改进到 Õ(k√d/(αε))，匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有差分隐私协方差估计在算子范数下的隐私相关样本复杂度存在额外 √k 因子（Õ(k^{3/2}√d/(αε))），与已知下界不匹配，需要消除该差距。

Method: 提出多尺度随机阈值算法；核心技术是直接对理想重构的中心化波动做算子范数界，利用条件独立性而非逐行累积逐项误差；通过多尺度阈值测试分配平衡重构方差与查询敏感度。

Result: 样本复杂度为 Õ(k^2/α^2 + k√d/(αε))，误差至多 ασ^2；相比已有上界在隐私相关项上改进 √k 因子，并在适用参数范围内匹配下界 Ω̃(k^2/α^2 + k√d/(αε))。

Conclusion: 通过直接算子范数波动界与多尺度阈值分配，消去了隐私相关样本复杂度中的额外 √k 因子，优化了近似误差与隐私保护之间的权衡。

Abstract: We study differentially private covariance estimation in operator norm for mean-zero sub-Gaussian distributions with unknown covariance support and at most $k$ nonzero entries per row. We develop a multiscale random-threshold algorithm with sample complexity $\ot(k^2/α^2+k\sqrt d/(α\varepsilon))$ for $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy and error at most $ασ^2$, where $d$ is the dimension and $σ$ is a known sub-Gaussian scale. The bound improves the privacy-dependent term of the existing $\ot(k^2/α^2+k^{3/2}\sqrt d/(α\varepsilon))$ \citep{kumar2026curse} upper bound by a factor of $\sqrt k$, and matches the lower bound of $\widetildeΩ(k^2/α^2 + k\sqrt{d}/(α\varepsilon))$ in its applicable parameter regime.
  Our key technical ingredient is a direct operator-norm bound on the centered fluctuations of an ideal reconstruction, exploiting conditional independence rather than accumulating entrywise errors across each row. A multiscale allocation of threshold tests balances reconstruction variance against query sensitivity. Together, these ingredients sharpen the trade-off between approximation error and privacy protection, removing the additional $\sqrt{k}$ factor from the privacy-dependent sample complexity.

</details>


### [240] [Robust Market Making with Hawkes Order Flow and Price Impact via Adversarial Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22785)
*Hao Yang,Zhenguo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将对抗式强化学习做市方法扩展到含Hawkes自激订单到达与交易诱导价格冲击的更复杂环境，并引入LSTM建模时序结构，通过博弈论分析与数值实验刻画均衡性质，提出关注收益左尾的鲁棒性评估协议；实验表明该方法在多数复杂微观结构环境下左尾表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实限价订单簿做市面临显著的模型不确定性与机制转换风险。现有对抗式强化学习虽将Avellaneda--Stoikov做市问题建模为做市商与环境对手的零和博弈以提升鲁棒性，但通常依赖泊松订单到达并忽略交易诱导的价格冲击，难以刻画订单流聚集、自激和交易后价格反馈等高频微观结构效应。

Method: 将对抗式强化学习做市扩展到含Hawkes自激订单到达与交易诱导价格冲击的环境；为缓解扩展机制空间带来的非平稳性，加入LSTM模块显式建模近期观测的时序结构；通过博弈论分析与数值实验刻画所提框架的均衡性质，并提出聚焦收益分布左尾改进的鲁棒性评估协议。

Result: 在一系列市场机制下的实验显示，所提方法在多数复杂微观结构环境中取得更优的左尾表现，尤其在Hawkes激发强、价格冲击低至中等的机制中增益显著。Bootstrap检验未发现这些改进来自更强的终端方向性库存偏差。

Conclusion: 将对抗训练与时序状态表示相结合，可提升基于强化学习的做市策略在订单流自激、价格冲击与机制不确定性下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Market-making strategies in real limit order book markets face substantial model uncertainty and regime-shift risk. Existing adversarial reinforcement learning approaches improve robustness by formulating the Avellaneda--Stoikov market-making problem as a zero-sum game between a market maker and an environmental adversary. However, these approaches typically rely on Poisson order arrivals and neglect trade-induced price impact, limiting their ability to capture important high-frequency market microstructure effects such as clustered order flow, self-excitation, and post-trade price feedback.
  We extend adversarial reinforcement learning for market making to a more complex environment with Hawkes self-exciting order arrivals and trade-induced price impact. To mitigate the increased non-stationarity introduced by the expanded regime space, we incorporate an LSTM module that explicitly models the temporal structure of recent observations. We further characterize the equilibrium properties of the proposed framework through both game-theoretic analysis and numerical experiments, and introduce a robustness evaluation protocol focused on improvements in the left tail of the return distribution.
  Experimental results across a range of market regimes show that the proposed method achieves improved left-tail performance in most complex microstructure environments. In particular, the gains are pronounced in regimes with strong Hawkes excitation and low-to-moderate price impact. Bootstrap tests provide no evidence that these improvements are obtained through a stronger terminal directional inventory bias. These results suggest that combining adversarial training with temporal state representation can improve the robustness of reinforcement-learning-based market-making strategies under order-flow self-excitation, price impact, and regime uncertainty.

</details>


### [241] [FIRM-WM: State-factorized factual-interventional recurrent modeling for reward-free visual planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22816)
*Yilun Wu,Yunjian Zhang,Aobo Li,Mujiangshan Wang,Haitao Wu,Aqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FIRM-WM通过分离"目标可比配置"与"动态纤维"的状态设计，并结合常见重置干预分支，提升了无奖励潜在世界模型在图像目标任务上的规划性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无奖励潜在世界模型需从离线视频学习并规划新图像目标任务，但其规划状态面临两个缺口：一是状态坐标需与目标图像可比，二是状态动态需保留速度、运动趋势、接触等历史相关信息；同时离线训练只提供单一事实未来，而采样式规划器需比较同一状态下未执行的动作。

Method: 提出FIRM-WM（事实-干预循环世界模型），其循环状态将类型化的目标可比配置与128维动态纤维分离，后者用于预测但不参与终端目标代价；利用广泛的事实轨迹提供状态覆盖，并通过常见重置干预分支获取替代动作序列的观测结果，在执行分支前重置环境并恢复环境状态设置接口暴露的相同记录值。

Result: 在匹配CEM规划及三个独立全流程种子下，FIRM-WM在TwoRoom达99.0±1.0%，Reacher达92.7±2.1%，OGBench-Cube达88.0±3.0%，而LeWM分别为89.0%、88.0%、70.0%；部署模型参数为2.98–3.42M，规划时间降低2.13–11.60×。

Conclusion: 通过状态分离设计与事实-干预数据机制，FIRM-WM在多个图像目标规划任务上取得更高成功率并显著降低规划时间，验证了其针对离线规划两大缺口的有效性。

Abstract: Reward-free latent world models can learn from offline videos and solve new image--goal tasks by optimizing actions through predicted latent futures. This setting places two demands on the planning state: its coordinates must be comparable with a goal image. Moreover, its dynamics must retain velocity, motion trend, contact, and other history--dependent information beyond those goal coordinates. Offline training creates a second mismatch: each recorded trajectory reveals one factual future, whereas a sampling--based planner compares many actions that were not taken from the same state. We introduce FIRM-WM (Factual--Interventional Recurrent World Model), a compact pixel world model designed around these two gaps. Its recurrent state separates a typed, goal--comparable configuration from a 128-dimensional dynamic fiber used for prediction but excluded from the terminal goal cost. Broad factual trajectories provide state coverage, while common--reset intervention branches provide observed outcomes for alternative action sequences. Before executing each branch, we reset the environment and restore the same recorded values exposed by the environment's state--setting interface. Under matched CEM planning and three independent full-pipeline seeds, FIRM-WM reaches 99.0$\pm$1.0% on TwoRoom, 92.7$\pm$2.1% on Reacher, and 88.0$\pm$3.0% on OGBench-Cube, compared with 89.0%, 88.0%, and 70.0% for LeWM. The deployed model uses 2.98--3.42M parameters and records 2.13--11.60$\times$ lower planning time on these tasks.

</details>


### [242] [Counterfactual Tool Ranking under Utility, Cost, and Privilege Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22819)
*Jiapeng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可证伪的反事实工具评估方法，通过11个企业场景工具、精确倾向日志和真实本地 MCP 传输，对直接回归与双重稳健（DR）估计进行系统检验，并用 BFCL 独立任务与本地模型验证，强调区分权威性、历史支持与比较目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实工具评估容易混淆三类问题：评估的权威性、历史数据支持程度、以及比较实际估计的对象。作者希望厘清这些区别，避免在缺乏支持的条件下错误宣称部署改进。

Method: 使用11个可执行的企业启发式工具、精确倾向日志和真实本地 MCP 传输；先做45次合成运行，再用30次实际回报控制运行、15个实验覆盖1930个 BFCL 任务；比较直接回归与 DR；在环境偏移、函数名分组不相交划分及缺失支持条件下分析策略差异；并用两个固定本地 Qwen2.5 模型在200个留出任务上评估。

Result: 全回报直接回归在线性设定下逆转了 DR 的初始有利结果：MAE 为直接回归 0.0139、DR 0.0272；环境偏移下 DR 占优，误差 0.0227 对 0.0948；BFCL 分组不相交划分上直接与 DR 选择器平衡准确率分别为 81.85% 和 79.83%；固定提示下本地模型表现出强烈的不弃答失败。缺失支持时，两策略共有支持动作相消，可在绝对值不可识别时点识别增量变化；保留分歧的回退在全部五个支持缺口运行中实现该性质，但保守采样界无法认证部署改进。

Conclusion: 贡献在于可证伪的评估方法与独立公开证据，而非新 DR 估计器、官方 BFCL 榜单成绩或生产智能体安全声明。

Abstract: Counterfactual tool evaluation must distinguish authority, historical support, and what a comparison actually estimates. We study these distinctions with eleven executable enterprise-inspired tools, exact-propensity logs, and real local Model Context Protocol transport. An initial 45-run synthetic study is retained, then challenged by 30 realized-return control runs and 15 experiments on 1,930 independently released Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard (BFCL) tasks. Full-return direct regression reverses an initially favorable doubly robust (DR) evaluation result in the linear setting: mean absolute errors are 0.0139 for direct regression and 0.0272 for DR. Under a shifted environment, DR retains an advantage, with errors 0.0227 versus 0.0948. On function-name-group-disjoint BFCL-derived splits, direct and DR selectors obtain balanced accuracies of 81.85% and 79.83%. Two pinned local Qwen2.5 models are evaluated on the same 200 held-out tasks, exposing a strong failure to abstain under the fixed prompt. We further characterize policy differences under missing support: unsupported actions shared by two policies cancel, allowing point identification of an incremental change when neither absolute value is identifiable. A disagreement-preserving fallback achieves this property in all five support-gap runs, but conservative sampling bounds do not certify deployment improvement. The contribution is a falsifiable evaluation method and independent public evidence, not a new DR estimator, official BFCL leaderboard score, or production-agent safety claim.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond Average Error through Oracle-Informed Stress Tests for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22820)
*Xu Lin,Runheng Zuo,Shengxuan Xu,Qitai Tan,Hongyu Lin,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套机制可控的配对压力测试基准，将期望平方误差变化分解为环境风险与预测-预言机距离两部分，并在24个可部署预测器、多变量关系偏移及独立DGP复现审计上进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均平方误差无法区分预测性能下降是因未来变得更难预测（环境风险），还是因预测偏离条件均值（信息差距）。需要一种能按机制归因的评估方法。

Method: 引入配对、机制可控的压力测试，在每个预测提前期将期望平方误差变化分解为环境风险与预测-预言机距离，使用评估模型无法获得的、以起点为条件的预测预言机；设置零假设、仅环境、信息缺口三种端到端控制；在24个可部署预测器上测试；研究多变量关系偏移（6个模型×3种耦合强度）；并对独立DGP实现进行预注册对比与稳定性审计。

Result: 频繁切换下，14种方法实现MSE更高但预言机距离更低；离群方差反馈下，19种MSE更高但尺度标准化MSE更低；短/长提前期压力响应排名Spearman相关仅0.624。多变量偏移中，预言机距离仅占分解风险增加的0.7–3.9%，环境风险占主导，且在八通道系统及Ring/Block/Hub关系审计中持续；若干视觉上显著的发现在独立数据上无法复现。

Conclusion: 该基准结合了分量诊断与留出稳定性审计，能揭示按机制归因的性能退化，并作为真实数据分布外评估的补充，后者在无法获得精确预言机归因时衡量现实偏移下的表现。

Abstract: Average squared error cannot reveal whether forecasting performance degrades because the future becomes less predictable or because forecasts move farther from the conditional mean. We introduce paired, mechanism-controlled stress tests that decompose changes in expected squared error at each lead time into environmental risk and forecast-oracle distance, using an origin-conditioned predictive oracle unavailable to the evaluated models. Three end-to-end controls have known attribution. Specifically, the null, environmental-only, and information-gap controls verify that the pipeline assigns changes to the correct component. We then apply the benchmark to 24 deployable forecasters. Under frequent switching, 14 methods have higher realized MSE but lower oracle distance; under outlier-variance feedback, 19 have higher MSE but lower scale-standardized MSE. Short- and long-lead stress-response rankings have Spearman correlation 0.624, revealing substantial horizon-dependent reordering. We then study multivariate relation shifts. Across six models and three coupling severities, oracle distance accounts for only 0.7-3.9% of the decomposed expected-risk increase, and environmental-risk majority persists in an eight-channel system and a matched-difficulty audit of Ring, Block, and Hub relations. Finally, prespecified contrasts on independent data-generating process (DGP) realizations show that several visually compelling discovery profiles, including trend accumulation and the hypothesized switching reversal, do not replicate. The benchmark thus combines component-wise diagnosis with a held-out stability audit. It complements real-data out-of-distribution evaluation, which measures performance under realistic shifts when exact oracle attribution is unavailable.

</details>


### [244] [Personalized Federated Reinforcement Learning via Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning: Convergence of Exact and Hessian-Free Meta-Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22833)
*Ali Beikmohammadi,Sarit Khirirat,Sindri Magnússon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究个性化联邦强化学习，提出 Per-FedAvg-PG 算法：n 个智能体在各自 MDP 中通过服务器协作学习一个 MAML 风格共享策略初始化，单个智能体仅需一步局部策略梯度即可适配。理论证明该算法在 K=O(ε^{-3/2}) 轮、每轮 τ=Θ(ε^{-1/2}) 局部步下达到 ε-近似一阶稳定点，并分析了无 Hessian 变体的偏差与稳定化下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦强化学习需要兼顾智能体间的协作与个体异质性；已有监督学习中的联邦元学习理论依赖有界梯度和有界异质性假设，这些假设在 RL 中是否自动成立、以及精确元梯度所需的内层策略 Hessian 带来的计算瓶颈，尚缺乏系统分析。

Method: 提出 Per-FedAvg-PG：智能体在通信轮之间执行 τ 步局部随机元策略梯度更新，学习一个共享的 MAML 风格策略初始化。利用标准策略类正则性条件，证明每个智能体的目标函数梯度与 Hessian 一致有界且常数显式，从而使监督理论中的有界梯度和有界异质性条件自动成立。此外分析无 Hessian 变体，界定其偏差，并构造元梯度非零且量级为 α 的不动点。
