Result: 使用基于人物角色的LLM模拟（具备用户角色的AI智能体预测个体行为）作为跨越两种粒度的预测来源，在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，方法结合基于人物角色的预测能大幅降低实验成本，同时保持严格的统计有效性。

Conclusion: 该学习增强假设检验框架能够有效利用未知质量的预测信号，在维持统计有效性和鲁棒性的同时显著减少A/B测试的样本量与成本，为实验设计提供了一种原则性的解决方案。

Abstract: A/B testing requires large sample sizes, long timelines, and significant costs. When auxiliary predictions of experimental outcomes are available from machine learning models, uncertain prediction quality precludes replacing human experiments entirely, yet these predictions may still contain useful signal. We propose a principled framework for learning-augmented hypothesis testing that leverages predictions of unknown quality to reduce sample sizes while maintaining statistical validity. Predictions naturally vary in granularity, from coarse aggregate signals to fine-grained individual-level estimates, and our framework addresses both ends of this spectrum: (1) for population-level directional predictions, where only a binary signal on the treatment effect sign is available, we use an asymmetric test and prove consistency and robustness bounds within the learning-augmented algorithms paradigm; (2) for individual-level predictions, we introduce Generalized PPI++ (GPPI), extending Prediction-Powered Inference to handle nonlinear prediction errors through higher-dimensional transformations. Both methods benefit from accurate predictions while remaining robust to inaccurate or adversarial ones. We validate our framework using persona-based LLM simulations, where AI agents equipped with user personas predict individual behavior, as a natural prediction source spanning both granularity levels. Experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that our methods, combined with persona-based predictions, substantially reduce experimental costs while preserving rigorous statistical validity.

</details>


### [349] [iSDFT: Information-Proximal Self-Distillation for Continual Learning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24646)
*Ahmed Khaled Khamis,Xiaotong Ji,Hassan Jaber,Rasul Tutunov,Matthieu Zimmer,Jun Wang,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 iSDFT，将教师视为有预算的信息源，在每个 token 处选择满足教师信息约束、且最接近当前学生的分布，得到闭式指数目标，并将学生锚定到冻结基策略以控制累积漂移，从而在专业化与保持通用能力间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy self-distillation fine-tuning（SDFT）虽能从演示中学习新技能并减少遗忘，但它总是向完整的"演示条件教师"蒸馏，把教师影响固定在满强度端点上，无法控制在每个预测状态下应转移多少演示信息。

Method: 提出 Information-Proximal SDFT（iSDFT），把教师视为有预算的信息源：在每个 token 处，选择满足指定教师信息约束、且与当前学生最接近的分布，得到带局部确定倾斜（tilt）的闭式指数目标；同时将学生锚定到其冻结的基础策略，以控制累积漂移。

Result: 在四个异构 LLM 骨干和两个专业化任务上，iSDFT 在 8 个模型-任务设置中的 7 个优于 vanilla SDFT，剩余 1 个持平；在原始 SDFT 基准套件上保持性更紧，73% 的评测保持在基模型 0.5 分以内（最强基线为 52%）；在全部十个额外的数学、代码和竞赛数学基准上取得最大的平均提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，控制教师信息引入的"量"与"时机"能够在提升专业化的同时保留更广泛的通用能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation fine-tuning (SDFT) learns new skills from demonstrations while reducing forgetting, but it always distils toward the full demonstration-conditioned teacher. This fixes teacher influence at the full-teacher endpoint, providing no control over how much demonstration information should be transferred at each prediction state. We introduce Information-Proximal SDFT (iSDFT), which instead treats the teacher as a budgeted source of information. At each token, iSDFT selects the distribution closest to the current student that satisfies a prescribed teacher-information constraint, yielding a closed-form exponential target with a locally determined tilt. To control cumulative drift, we further anchor the student to its frozen base policy. Across four heterogeneous LLM backbones and two specialisation tasks, iSDFT improves vanilla SDFT in 7 of 8 model-task settings and matches it in the remaining one. It also provides tighter retention on the original SDFT benchmark suite, with 73% of evaluations remaining within 0.5 points of the base model versus 52% for the strongest baseline, while achieving the largest mean improvement on all ten additional mathematics, coding, and competition-mathematics benchmarks. These results show that controlling how much and when teacher information is introduced improves specialisation while preserving broader capability.

</details>


### [350] [SPeaR: Test-Time Adaptation with Steering Primitives for Realigning Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24111)
*Muhammad Sudipto Siam Dip,Ali Etemad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPeaR，冻结预训练网络，通过在阶段边界插入轻量可学习模块来引导中间表示，实现无需源数据和监督预热的测试时自适应，在多个基准上匹配或超越参数量大得多的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应（TTA）方法通常通过更新预训练模型参数来应对分布偏移，这限制了可自适应的内容和网络中可以发生自适应的位置。

Method: 保持预训练网络冻结，改为引导其中间表示。在阶段边界插入轻量可学习模块（Steering Primitive），直接从测试流优化，无需源数据或监督预热。每个模块采用门控目标，仅在自适应有益时降低不确定性；配合多样性正则化防止坍缩，并使用多深度锚点稳定自适应。

Result: 引导早期表示最为有效；同一模块可在卷积与 Transformer 架构间迁移。在 CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C 和 ImageNet-C 上，SPeaR 稳定匹配或超越参数量多出数个数量级的方法，在广泛批次大小下保持稳健，并在持续自适应中保持源域性能。

Conclusion: 通过冻结网络并引导中间表示，SPeaR 以极少参数实现高效、稳健的测试时自适应，并具备跨架构可迁移性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) addresses distribution shift using only unlabeled test data. Existing methods typically adapt pretrained models by updating their parameters, limiting both what is adapted and where adaptation can occur within the network. We instead keep the pretrained network frozen and steer its intermediate representations. We introduce SPeaR (Steering Primitive for Realigning Representations), which inserts lightweight learnable modules at stage boundaries and optimizes them directly from the test stream, requiring neither source data nor supervised warm-up. Each primitive is optimized using a gated objective that reduces uncertainty only when adaptation is beneficial, along with a diversity regularizer to prevent collapse, and a multi-depth anchor to stabilize adaptation. We show that steering early representations is the most effective strategy, and that the same primitive transfers across convolutional and Transformer architectures. Across CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-C, SPeaR consistently matches or outperforms methods that adapt orders of magnitude more parameters, remains robust across a wide range of batch sizes, and preserves source-domain performance during continual adaptation.

</details>


### [351] [Corrective Forcing: Unified Post-Training for Diffusions and Flows in Generative Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24651)
*Qing Yao,Lijian Gao,Qirong Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Corrective Forcing（CoF）后训练范式，让扩散与流模型在自生成 rollout 状态上学习纠正预测，缓解训练-推理不匹配，提升语音增强的质量与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流模型用于语音增强时存在训练-推理不匹配：训练使用解析路径状态，而推理沿离散采样轨迹递归地在自生成状态上评估模型，导致预测误差与离散化误差不断累积。

Method: CoF 是一种后训练范式：在动态采样调度下，将 rollout 状态上的干净语音预测朝真实标签纠正，使模型暴露于多样的推理条件；同时以局部纠正的反事实转移作为真实转移的参考，正则化局部演化。通过共享的干净语音预测参数化表达模型输出，使同一后训练目标适用于扩散与流两类形式。

Result: 在 SB-VE 与 OT-CFM 上的实验表明，感知质量与重建保真度均有提升，并在不同采样步数下保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: CoF 通过自生成 rollout 上的纠正式后训练，有效缓解训练-推理不匹配与误差累积，为扩散和流模型在语音增强中提供统一且鲁棒的后训练方案。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow models, as promising generative paradigms for speech enhancement, face a training--inference mismatch: training uses analytical path states, whereas inference recursively evaluates models on self-generated rollout states along discretized sampling trajectories. This mismatch causes prediction and discretization errors to accumulate. To address it, we introduce Corrective Forcing (CoF), a post-training paradigm that forces diffusion and flow models to learn from self-generated rollouts and correct their predictions. CoF corrects clean-speech predictions on rollout states toward the ground truth under dynamic sampling schedules, exposing the model to varying inference conditions. It further regularizes local evolution using locally corrected counterfactual transitions as references for factual transitions. By expressing model outputs through a shared clean-speech prediction parameterization, CoF applies the same post-training objective across diffusion and flow formulations. Experiments with SB-VE and OT-CFM demonstrate improvements in perceptual quality and reconstruction fidelity, together with robust performance across different numbers of sampling steps.

</details>


### [352] [PAC-Bayesian Meta-Learning for Few-Shot Identification of Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24117)
*Chenfeng Huang,George Michailidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PBML-LTI，一个面向少样本LTI系统辨识的PAC-Bayesian元学习框架，学习可迁移先验并保留任务异质性，通过鞅PAC-Bayes分析解决轨迹时序依赖问题，给出支持-查询预测风险界及fit-KL元训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当轨迹短、含噪或高维时，LTI系统辨识困难。传统系统辨识独立处理每个系统，无法利用相关系统间的共享结构。同时，现有PAC-Bayes元学习分析多假设i.i.d.，而LTI轨迹存在时序依赖，导致理论不适用。

Method: 将每个任务视为未知LTI系统，元学习器用训练轨迹学习转移矩阵的数据依赖先验；新系统数据有限时在该先验下做贝叶斯自适应得到任务相关后验，实现估计与不确定性量化。针对时序依赖，采用鞅PAC-Bayes分析处理依赖轨迹损失，推导支持-查询预测风险界，并据此提出fit-KL元训练目标，进一步给出转移矩阵恢复与多步轨迹预测的推论。

Result: 得到依赖轨迹损失下的支持-查询预测风险界，明确经验拟合、后验复杂度与先验质量在序列依赖下少样本自适应中的作用；并导出转移矩阵恢复和多步轨迹预测的有限样本保证推论。

Conclusion: PBML-LTI在保留任务异质性的同时实现可迁移先验学习，为依赖动态数据提供不确定性感知的元辨识与有限样本理论保证，连接了元学习与依赖数据的泛化分析。

Abstract: Identifying linear time-invariant (LTI) dynamical systems is challenging when trajectories are short, noisy, or high-dimensional. Traditional system identification typically treats each system independently and cannot exploit shared structure across related systems. We propose PBML-LTI, a PAC-Bayesian meta-learning framework for few-shot LTI system identification that learns a transferable prior over task-specific dynamics while preserving task heterogeneity. Each task corresponds to an unknown LTI system, and the meta-learner uses training trajectories to learn a data-dependent prior over transition matrices. For a new system with limited data, PBML-LTI performs Bayesian adaptation under this prior to obtain a task-specific posterior, providing accurate estimates and principled uncertainty quantification.
  A key challenge is temporal dependence, since LTI trajectories violate the i.i.d. assumptions underlying most PAC-Bayes meta-learning analyses. We address this with a martingale PAC-Bayes analysis for dependent trajectory losses and derive a support-query predictive-risk bound that motivates a fit-KL meta-training objective. The bound clarifies the roles of empirical fit, posterior complexity, and prior quality in few-shot adaptation under sequential dependence. We further derive corollaries for transition-matrix recovery and multi-step trajectory prediction, connecting uncertainty-aware meta-identification with finite-sample guarantees for dependent dynamical data.

</details>


### [353] [CLOOPD: Closing the Learner Loop in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24141)
*Keye Zheng,Hanyu Li,Zhan Cheng,Yuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLOOPD，一个将教师信号获取与学生端实现分离的闭环在线策略蒸馏框架，通过自适应α航点、冻结已评分批次及多遍actor更新，在同等教师token规模下显著提升蒸馏效果并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中，学生生成轨迹、教师打分，每个批次需付出双重代价。现有方法多聚焦于"给哪些轨迹打分"和"如何构造教师信号"，但通常只用一次actor更新来消耗这些信号，导致教师token利用不充分、学习不彻底。

Method: CLOOPD将教师信号获取与学生端实现分离：在KL包络内选取自适应α航点，冻结已评分的批次及其优势值，每次actor更新后重新前向学生网络、衡量实现程度，并在独立的token预算下分配actor工作量。框架包含确定性的两遍与三遍策略、按token计价的CLOOPD-TPMR，以及预算匹配的对照组。

Result: 在8×H20节点上六次300步实验中，所有CLOOPD策略在可比教师token规模下均优于单遍TOP-D基线：宏准确率从15.41提升至17.78（CLOOPD-Fixed2）和19.36（CLOOPD-Fixed3）。第100步时，CLOOPD-Fixed3达到15.35，接近TOP-D第300步水平，同时教师评分token减少67.2%、GPU小时减少28.0%。早期8×A100消融显示自适应α消除了观察到的信任包络违规，第三遍带来额外提升空间。

Conclusion: CLOOPD为"如何预算学生从教师评分token中学习的程度"提供了一个通用框架，在提升蒸馏效果的同时显著降低教师评分token与GPU计算开销。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) pays twice for each fresh batch: the student generates trajectories and a stronger teacher scores them. Existing methods improve which trajectories are scored and how the teacher signal is constructed, but usually consume it with one actor update. We introduce CLOOPD, a closed-loop framework separating teacher-signal acquisition from student-side realization. CLOOPD selects an adaptive $α$ waypoint inside a KL envelope, freezes the scored batch and its advantages, re-forwards the student after each actor pass, measures realization, and allocates actor work under a separate token budget. The framework includes deterministic two- and three-pass policies, token-priced CLOOPD-TPMR, and a budget-matched control. Across six 300-step runs on an 8-H20 node, every CLOOPD policy improves the one-pass TOP-D anchor at comparable teacher-token scale: macro accuracy rises from 15.41 to 17.78 with CLOOPD-Fixed2 and 19.36 with CLOOPD-Fixed3. At step 100, CLOOPD-Fixed3 reaches 15.35, nearly matching TOP-D at step 300 while using 67.2% fewer teacher-scored tokens and 28.0% fewer GPU-hours. Earlier 8-A100 ablations show adaptive $α$ eliminates observed trust-envelope violations; a third pass adds headroom. These results position CLOOPD as a framework for budgeting how fully students learn from teacher-scored tokens.

</details>


### [354] [Enhancing Transformer Representations of Symbolic ODE Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24746)
*Xiyue Fan,Adam Prugel-Bennett,Stuart E. Middleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究树位置嵌入在符号常微分方程（ODE）求解任务中的作用，并引入对比学习以支持数学交换性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解微分方程的方法（符号回归、物理信息神经网络、神经算子）多聚焦数值近似或基于数值数据的盲目符号搜索，较少关注保留交换性质、支持符号形式数学推理的结构化表示。Transformer 虽在符号微分方程上表现强，但其标准位置嵌入针对序列数据设计，而符号微分方程天然是表达式树结构，难以高效捕捉层次结构。

Method: 在符号 ODE 任务中系统研究现有树位置嵌入的有效性，测试不同设置；基于学到的结构表示，应用对比学习支持数学中的交换性质；并通过消融实验分析各方法的相互作用。

Result: 树位置嵌入有助于早期轮次的学习，并在整个训练过程中持续提升性能，在不同数据规模和任务上均取得一致优势。消融实验揭示了这些方法在建模符号数学结构时的相互作用。

Conclusion: 树位置嵌入能更有效地捕捉符号微分方程的层次结构，结合对比学习可支持数学交换性质，为符号形式数学推理提供结构化表示基础。

Abstract: Existing approaches to solving differential equations, such as symbolic regression, physics informed neural networks, and neural operators, typically focus on numerical approximations or blind symbolic search via fitting to numerical data. Less attention has been paid to learning structured representations of mathematical expressions that preserve commutative properties and could support mathematical reasoning in symbolic forms. Transformer models have shown strong capabilities in solving symbolic differential equations. However, standard positional embeddings in transformers are designed for sequence data. Symbolic differential equations are naturally represented by expression trees, so these positional embeddings may not efficiently capture their hierarchical structures. We investigate existing tree positional embeddings in symbolic ordinary differential equation (ODE) tasks. We systematically study their effectiveness under different settings. Our results show that tree positional embeddings aid learning in early epochs and continue to improve performance throughout, ultimately yielding consistent advantages across various data sizes and tasks. Based on learned structural representations, we apply contrastive learning to support the commutative property in mathematics. Ablation studies provide insight into how these methods interact in modelling symbolic mathematical structures.

</details>


### [355] [Luck Is Not Skill: When Do Paired Rollouts Help Group-Relative RL of LLM Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24144)
*Nazmus Sakib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究组相对强化学习中的配对轨迹（paired rollouts）：组内共享事件键控噪声调度，以消除调度间奖励对比方差。理论上给出单侧评分噪声下方差降低的精确条件及反例，实验在2B工具使用智能体的工具故障与评分翻转下进行。工具故障下配对使最终噪声测试成功率平均提升5.1个百分点，但未达预注册学习曲线标准；梯度探测显示两种噪声下均出现更低的中心化协方差迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习通过比较同一提示的多条轨迹来估计优势，但独立的环境噪声会干扰这种比较，混淆奖励对比。作者希望考察配对轨迹（组内共享噪声调度、同时保持各轨迹边际分布不变）能否降低奖励对比方差，并厘清「改善奖励对比」「降低估计量方差」与「提升学习速度」之间的区别。

Method: 1）理论分析：研究配对轨迹对奖励对比方差与梯度方差的影响，针对单侧评分噪声推导方差降低的精确条件，并构造反例。2）受控实验：训练2B工具使用智能体，在工具故障与评分翻转两类噪声下，每种设计使用三个随机种子；协议预注册并披露了此前已完成的试点。3）梯度探测：在来自两条故障训练轨迹的八个不同检查点上测量中心化协方差迹。

Result: 理论上，配对消除了调度间奖励对比方差分量，但不必然降低梯度方差；对单侧评分噪声给出精确的降低条件，并给出奖励对比改善而梯度方差增大的反例。实验上，工具故障下配对使最终噪声测试成功率平均提升+5.1个百分点，且三个种子的差异均为正，但未达到预注册的学习曲线标准。评分翻转下同样未达标：验证AUC差值为+0.003（95%区间[-0.029,+0.033]）。梯度探测显示两种噪声下中心化协方差迹均更低：评分翻转为21%–30%，工具故障为40%–63%。

Conclusion: 有限样本测量支持配对背后的方差机制，但不足以确立普遍的学习速度收益。研究结果区分了三件事：改善奖励对比、降低估计量方差与提升实际学习效果，三者并不等价。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning compares rollouts of the same prompt, but independent environment noise can obscure these comparisons. We study paired rollouts, which share an event-keyed noise schedule within each group while preserving each rollout's marginal distribution. Pairing removes the between-schedule component of reward-contrast variance, but need not reduce gradient variance. For one-sided grader noise, we derive an exact condition for reduction and give a counterexample in which reward contrasts improve while gradient variance increases. A controlled study trains a 2B tool-use agent under tool faults and grader flips, with three seeds per design. The protocol was registered with a disclosed, previously completed pilot. Under tool faults, pairing improves final noisy-test success by +5.1 percentage points on average, with all three seed differences positive, but misses the registered learning-curve criterion. The criterion is also missed under grader flips: the validation-AUC difference is +0.003 (95% interval [-0.029, +0.033]). A gradient probe on eight distinct checkpoints from two fault-trained trajectories finds lower mean-centered covariance traces under both noise types: 21 to 30% for grader flips and 40 to 63% for tool faults. These finite-sample measurements support the variance mechanism without establishing a general learning-speed benefit. The results distinguish improving reward comparisons, reducing estimator variance, and improving learning.

</details>


### [356] [Mind or Message? Auditing Theory of Mind in Multi-Agent Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24146)
*Cong Li,Cheng Chen,Thomas Fung,Alex Rossi,Yi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过40个谈判任务、160个对话组和2880次反事实探测，检验语言模型智能体在社交模拟中是基于“心理模型”还是“对话表层记录”来理解伙伴。结果显示智能体社交流利但经济低效，其推断主要源于自我中心投射而非真正的伙伴建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体被广泛用于模拟社会互动，其对话记录看似表明智能体彼此理解。作者试图探究这种“理解”究竟建立在伙伴心理模型之上，还是仅依赖伙伴话语的表层记录。

Method: 构建一个社会模拟环境，包含40个多议题谈判，隐藏偏好权重与完整帕累托前沿均为已知构造。两个模型家族在160个对话组中谈判，所有记录在测量前冻结。随后进行2880次反事实探测，在保持证据逐字节相同的情况下，逐一改变读者自身利益、伙伴语气、身份标签和递归顺序等因素。

Result: 智能体社交流利但经济表现差：96.2%对话组达成协议且无协议失败，但仅0.7%的交易落在帕累托前沿，20.5%的联合价值未被获取，76.6%的交易错过了双方利益完全一致的那个议题。仅更换读者自身收益表就使推断的首要优先级移动15.0个百分点，语气改写移动5.3个百分点，身份标签为0.0。智能体预测伙伴对自己的信念准确率为72.5%，而伙伴信念本身正确率仅51.2%。

Conclusion: 智能体的社交理解更多依赖对话表层而非伙伴心理模型，表现为自我中心投射而非真正推断。它们追踪对话的能力远强于追踪对话背后的心智，因而看似理解实则经济低效。

Abstract: Language model agents are increasingly used to simulate social interaction, and the resulting transcripts read as though the agents understand one another. We ask whether that appearance rests on a model of the partner's mind or on the surface record of what the partner said. We build a social simulation in which both questions have exact answers: 40 multi-issue negotiations whose hidden preference weights and whose full Pareto frontier are known by construction. Two model families negotiate across 160 dyads, every transcript is frozen before any measurement, and 2880 counterfactual probes then hold the evidence byte identical while moving one factor at a time: the reader's own stake, the partner's tone, an identity label, and the order of recursion. The agents are socially fluent and economically poor. They reach agreement in 96.2% of dyads with 0 protocol failures, yet only 0.7% of deals land on the Pareto frontier, they leave 20.5% of the available joint value unclaimed, and they miss the one issue on which their interests are perfectly aligned in 76.6% of deals; on the frontier and on that aligned issue, a package drawn at random from the set both sides would accept does as well. The probes locate the failure. Swapping only the reader's own payoff sheet, while the partner's words and offers stay identical, moves the inferred top priority by 15.0 percentage points, which is egocentric projection rather than inference, while a tone rewrite moves it by 5.3 percentage points and an identity label by 0.0. Most tellingly, an agent predicts what its partner believes about it 72.5% of the time while that partner's belief is itself correct only 51.2% of the time: the agents track the conversation far better than they track the mind behind it.

</details>


### [357] [When Tomorrow Becomes Today: Self-Evolving Policies for Agentic Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24862)
*Yifan Hu,Xilin Dai,Zhiyuan Qu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-ming Yang,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimEvolve：一种冻结骨干的时序智能体，通过“预测-揭示-更新”协议将部署中延迟反馈转化为专家信任、路径选择与干预强度的联合策略持续更新，在八个Time-MMD领域取得最优平均MSE/MAE排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序预测机制随时间演化，数值模型、推理策略与干预规则的相对有效性时变，因此智能体需自适应调整预测与编排策略。部署过程本身在预测期结束后会揭示真实目标，可提供监督而无需额外标注；但现有时间序列智能体多通过预测精修、反思或检索引入经验，未系统地把已实现结果转化为对后续起点的联合编排策略的持续更新。

Method: 提出TimEvolve，一种冻结骨干的时间序列智能体。在观察目标前提交全部数值专家预测与候选智能体路径，使每个已实现结果可评估整个备选集合，形成延迟反馈；采用按时间排序的“预测-揭示-更新”协议，将每个已实现结果转化为对专家信任、智能体路径选择与干预强度的持久联合更新，并应用于后续预测。

Result: 在八个Time-MMD领域、十五种方法的对比实验中，TimEvolve取得最佳平均MSE与MAE排名，并在七个领域上两项指标误差最低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从部署过程中遇到的实际未来结果中学习预测策略具有显著价值。

Abstract: Agentic time series forecasting concerns systems whose underlying mechanisms evolve, making the relative effectiveness of numerical models, reasoning strategies, and intervention rules inherently time-varying. Consequently, a time series agent must adapt the forecasts it produces and the orchestration policy that determines which components to trust and how to coordinate them. The deployment process naturally provides supervision for this adaptation as forecast horizons elapse and realized targets reveal the effectiveness of earlier decisions. Committing all numerical expert forecasts and candidate agent paths before target observation allows each realized outcome to evaluate the entire alternative set, providing delayed feedback without additional annotation. However, existing time series agents primarily incorporate prior experience through forecast refinement, reflection, or retrieval, without systematically converting realized outcomes into persistent updates to the joint orchestration policy governing later origins. To exploit this delayed feedback systematically, we introduce TimEvolve, a frozen-backbone time series agent that converts each realized outcome into persistent joint updates of expert trust, agent path selection, and intervention strength. A temporally ordered predict, reveal, and update protocol applies this feedback to subsequent forecasts. Experiments across eight Time-MMD domains show that TimEvolve achieves the best average MSE and MAE ranks among fifteen methods and the lowest errors on both metrics in seven domains. These results demonstrate the value of learning forecasting policies from the futures encountered during deployment.

</details>


### [358] [Acceptance-Aware Draft Model Training for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24150)
*Tianhua Xia,Mugilan Ganesan,Yifei Feng,Haiyu Wang,Maximilian Egger,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直接优化接受长度的训练损失：针对贪心验证的期望接受长度(EAL)损失和针对采样解码的窗口总变差(WTV)损失，并可结合GRPO强化学习阶段，实验表明其接受长度优于基于KL的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码的加速效果主要由接受长度决定，但现有草稿模型训练方法主要优化交叉熵或KL散度等代理目标，这些目标只鼓励分布匹配，并不直接优化接受长度；同时，贪心与采样解码下的接受机制也不同。

Method: 提出感知接受长度的训练损失，直接优化投机窗口内期望被接受的token数：针对贪心验证推导期望接受长度(EAL)损失以显式最大化期望接受长度；针对采样解码引入窗口总变差(WTV)损失，在考虑序列接受依赖的同时优化温度缩放后的草稿与目标分布重叠；两种目标还可结合以模拟接受长度为奖励的组相对强化学习(GRPO)阶段。

Result: 在不同目标模型与草稿模型、任务及解码设置下，所提损失相较基于KL的训练持续提升接受长度；WTV在采样解码下增益尤为显著，EAL则更契合贪心验证。

Conclusion: 直接优化接受目标，并针对具体解码模式定制损失，比传统的分布匹配目标更有效。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a lightweight draft model to generate multiple candidate tokens that are verified by the target model in a single forward pass. Its speedup is largely determined by the acceptance length, yet existing draft-model training methods mainly optimize cross-entropy or Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence as proxies. These objectives encourage distribution matching but do not directly optimize acceptance length, and the acceptance mechanism also differs between greedy and sampling-based decoding.
  In this work, we propose acceptance-length-aware training losses that directly optimize the expected number of accepted tokens within a speculative window. For greedy verification, we derive an expected accepted length (EAL) loss that explicitly maximizes expected acceptance length. For sampling-based decoding, we introduce a window total variation (WTV) loss that optimizes the overlap between temperature-scaled draft and target distributions while accounting for sequential acceptance dependencies. Both objectives can be further combined with a group-relative reinforcement learning stage (GRPO) using simulated acceptance length as the reward.
  Experiments across different target and draft models, tasks, and decoding settings show that our losses consistently improve acceptance length over KL-based training. WTV provides particularly strong gains under sampling-based decoding, while EAL better matches greedy verification. These results show that directly optimizing the acceptance objective, with losses tailored to the decoding mode, is more effective than conventional distribution-matching objectives.

</details>


### [359] [Exactness at Inference: A Representational Criterion for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24942)
*Filipe Marinho Rocha,Inês Dutra,Vítor Santos Costa,Luís Paulo Reis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一个关于模型泛化能力的理论判据：模型只有在计算与生成机制结构等价的表示时才能外推，否则只是拟合近似。作者用 Tensor Logic 说明零温张量收缩等价于离散逻辑，并据此区分不同方法的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关心模型为何在训练分布外失效。作者认为关键在于模型是否学到结构等价于生成机制的表示，而非拟合近似，这一等价性是分布内外精确性的必要条件，并可解释外推、混合架构传播以及归纳偏置来源等问题。

Method: 通过理论分析提出泛化判据：要求推断过程具备精确可表示性。以 Tensor Logic 为案例，将零温收缩对应离散逻辑，张量为布尔、嵌入为正交归一，仅算术连续；并比较逻辑张量网络、可微归纳逻辑编程、Tensor Logic 等在 $T=0$ 时是否通过该判据，讨论闭合域、Datalog 与 Prolog、外部记忆绑定新实体等问题。

Result: 该判据既不要求离散表示也不要求抽取式表达，只约束推断而非训练：取值在 $[0,1]$ 的精确边际可通过，而经阈值化为硬标签的神经网络不通过；逻辑张量网络不符合，可微归纳逻辑编程与 $T=0$ 的 Tensor Logic 符合。分段仿射外推发散与无法绑定新实体是精确可表示性不足的两个表现；混合架构的输出继承路径上每个拟合估计器的边界。在法则导出的划分上，精确假设类可识别远处查询中 56.3% 可回答者，而集成方法会给出错误置信，距离度量排序相反。

Conclusion: 从对称性到记忆等常见归纳偏置之所以能达到精确性，是因为人类注入；因此应诱导精确表示，而非拟合替代模型，因为替代模型即便训练时达到算术下限，其残差在数据外仍会发散并在复合下累积。

Abstract: A model generalizes outside its training distribution only when it computes a representation structurally equivalent to the generating mechanism, not an approximation fitted to it. Such equivalence is necessary for exactness in and out of distribution, and extrapolation is governed by this exactness at inference, whatever its realization. Tensor Logic shows this: a zero-temperature contraction is equivalent to discrete logic, deducing in place with no artefact extracted, its tensors Boolean, its embeddings orthonormal, only its arithmetic continuous. Lacking infinite recursion it reaches Datalog, not Prolog, and though exact over closed domains it needs external memory to bind a novel entity. The criterion needs neither a discrete representation nor an extracted expression, and constrains inference, not training: an exact marginal in $[0,1]$ passes, a Neural Network thresholded to a hard label does not. Logic Tensor Networks fail it, while differentiable ILP and Tensor Logic at $T=0$ pass. Piecewise-affine extrapolation divergence and an inability to bind novel entities are two faces of a shortfall in exact representability. For hybrid architectures, a propagation rule follows: the output inherits the bounds of every fitted estimator on its path, explaining which axes fail in equivariant models and the ARC-AGI induction/transduction split. Only an exact hypothesis class certifies what the training data leave underdetermined: on a law-derived partition it finds the $56.3\%$ of distant queries that are answerable, which ensembles meet with false confidence and distance metrics rank backwards. Common inductive biases, from symmetries to memory, reach exactness only because humans inject them, an argument for inducing exact representations rather than fitting surrogates whose residuals, even at the arithmetic floor in training, diverge outside the data and compound under composition.

</details>


### [360] [H-Spec: Parallel Speculative Decoding Without a Drafter-Side KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24197)
*Weifan Jiang,Krishna Teja Chitty-Venkata,Megan Flynn,Reed Meyerson,Zhenting Qi,Tianyu Wu,Eldar Kurtic,Minlan Yu,Alexandre Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合目标上下文注入方法与 H-Spec 并行草稿器，避免独立草稿 KV 缓存，在保持块并行的同时提升接受长度与推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块扩散草稿器将目标隐藏状态投影到独立的草稿侧 KV 缓存，导致随并发增长的内存与 KV 写入开销；而直接复用目标 KV 又难以在整个块内维持草稿质量。

Method: 混合目标上下文注入：仅对最后一个输入位置注入目标隐藏状态，配合直接复用目标 KV，无需独立草稿 KV 缓存；在此基础上构建 H-Spec 混合 Mamba-注意力并行草稿器，Mamba 模块由投影后的末位目标隐藏状态初始化，注意力模块就地复用目标 KV，并借助 Mamba 并行扫描保持块并行草稿。

Result: 在三个目标模型与多种任务上，较最优基线平均接受长度提升 5.0–13.3%，batch-size-1 的 token 间延迟加速提升 5.3–12.6%；并发服务下吞吐更高且 KV 缓存占用更低。

Conclusion: H-Spec 通过互补的上下文注入与就地 KV 复用，在无额外草稿缓存的前提下实现更优的草稿质量与推理效率，适合并发部署场景。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates large language model inference by having a lightweight draft model predict future tokens for verification by the target model. Recent block diffusion drafters further reduce drafting latency by predicting multiple tokens in parallel. However, existing block drafters project target hidden states at every input position into a separate drafter-side KV cache, incurring per-request memory and KV-write overhead that grow with concurrency; directly reusing target KVs in place removes this cache but fails to sustain draft quality throughout the block. We propose a hybrid target-context injection method that complements direct target KV reuse with target hidden states only at the last input position, requiring no separate drafter-side KV cache. Building on this design, we propose H-Spec, a hybrid Mamba-attention parallel drafter that consumes the two target-context sources through complementary modules. Mamba modules are initialized with projected last-token target hidden states, while attention modules reuse target KVs in place. Despite its recurrent formulation, Mamba's parallel scan allows H-Spec to preserve block-parallel drafting. Across three target models and diverse tasks, H-Spec improves over the best baseline by 5.0--13.3% in mean accepted length and 5.3--12.6% in batch-size-1 inter-token latency speedup. Under concurrent serving, H-Spec consistently achieves higher throughput while maintaining lower KV cache utilization than baselines across evaluated concurrency levels.

</details>


### [361] [Rare Event Estimation via Iterative Unalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24969)
*Hanming Yang,Daksh Mittal,Jing Dong,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于权重扰动的重要性采样方法，用于估计智能体随机输出轨迹中极罕见事件的发生概率，在概率低于$10^{-7}$的事件上相比朴素蒙特卡洛实现超过800倍的计算加权效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体部署自主性提高，其随机输出轨迹中即使极罕见的事件也可能带来灾难性后果，因此安全部署关注的关键不是事件是否可能发生，而是发生频率有多高。估计这类风险需要在组合上极其庞大的轨迹空间中搜索，朴素蒙特卡洛计算代价过高，而构造有效的重要性采样提议分布又需要协调调整一条依赖上下文的链式条件分布，因此亟需更高效的估计方法。

Method: 作者开发了一种新的重要性采样方法，通过扰动原模型的权重来构造提议分布。该提议分布本身是一个可微分参数化的语言模型，从而支持在权重空间中进行基于梯度的搜索。方法构建了一个目标函数，结合了用于事件放大的可微代理项，以及自适应正则化机制，动态地在放大效果与估计器稳定性之间取得平衡。

Result: 作者在约1.2亿和约26亿参数的模型上，跨三个事件家族、300多个概率低至$10^{-9}$的罕见事件进行了评估，参考概率以小于10%的相对标准误差计算。在最可验证的设置中，对于概率低于$10^{-7}$的事件，其重要性采样估计器相比朴素蒙特卡洛实现了超过800倍的计算加权效率提升。

Conclusion: 该工作表明通过扰动模型权重构造可微分提议分布并结合自适应正则化，能够有效、稳定地估计智能体自身随机行为导致的极罕见事件概率，显著优于朴素蒙特卡洛，为智能体安全部署中的风险评估提供了实用工具。实现已开源。

Abstract: As agents are deployed with increased autonomy, even extremely rare events along their stochastic output trajectories can occur and prove catastrophic. Safe deployment therefore does not depend on whether these events can occur, but on how often they might. We study the problem of estimating the probability of rare events that arise from stochastic variation in the agent's own actions. Estimating this type of risk requires searching over the combinatorially vast space of trajectories. Naive Monte Carlo is computationally prohibitive in this regime, and constructing effective importance sampling (IS) proposals requires coordinated changes to a context-dependent chain of conditional distributions. We develop a new IS method that perturbs the original model's weights to construct the proposal. The proposal is itself a differentiably parameterized language model, enabling gradient-based search over weight space. We formulate an objective that combines a differentiable surrogate for event amplification and an adaptive regularization scheme that dynamically balances amplification against estimator stability. We evaluate our approach on $\sim$120M and $\sim$2.6B models across three event families spanning 300+ rare events as rare as $10^{-9}$, with reference probabilities computed with $<10\%$ relative standard error. In our most verifiable settings, we observe that our IS estimator achieves over $800\times$ compute-weighted efficiency gains over naive Monte Carlo for events with probabilities lower than $10^{-7}$. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/namkoong-lab/iterative-unalignment.

</details>


### [362] [Displacement Geometry Captures Platonic Shared Reality Across Models and Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24209)
*Chenming Shang,Yujin Tang,Jun Jie Ou Yang,Ruize Xu,Adam Breuer,Nikhil Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型的共享之处不在于样本的绝对位置，而在于样本间的位移向量；在单一正交对齐下位移被跨模型保留，而绝对位置不保留。由此可用一次缓存前向传播把能力从一个模型导入另一个模型（Shadow Casting），并以SHADOWCLIP验证其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柏拉图表征假说（PRH）主张独立训练的模型会收敛到共享的现实现象统计模型，但近期研究发现模型之间仅存在微弱的逐点相似性。论文试图调和这一矛盾：解释模型究竟在哪一层面上共享表征，并借此实现能力的低成本迁移。

Method: 提出表征分解框架：表征可拆为线性对齐的共享语义成分与不可对齐的私有能力成分。在单一正交对齐（仅旋转与反射）下考察44个独立训练的视觉与语言编码器间的位移向量。进一步从概念级结构解释该几何：模型内父概念与其子变化向量正交，跨模型概念位移相互平行。并通过微调与关系蒸馏两类实验进行可证伪预测。基于此提出Shadow Casting，实例化为SHADOWCLIP。

Result: 在跨越模态与非对称能力配对的44个编码器上，位移向量在正交对齐下被显著保留（与PRH证据一致），而样本绝对位置不被保留（与近期反证一致）。实验证实微调保留逐点相似性但破坏位移，关系蒸馏则相反。SHADOWCLIP以数量级更少的计算量超越强微调基线。

Conclusion: 由于语义呈线性对齐而能力不然，能力可通过源模型的一次缓存前向传播导入其他模型。缓存可与开放权重一同发布，使一个模型的能力被下载并导入任意数量的其他模型而无需微调。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) claims that independently trained models converge on a shared statistical model of reality, yet recent work finds only weak pointwise similarity between models. In this paper, we show that what models share is not the location of samples in representation space, but the directions (displacement vectors) between them. Under a single orthogonal alignment--rotation and reflection only--these displacement vectors are substantially preserved across 44 independently trained vision and language encoders spanning modalities and asymmetric capability pairs, consistent with the PRH evidence. The samples' absolute positions are not, consistent with recent counter-evidence. Both arise from a single decomposition: representations split into a shared semantic component that is linearly aligned across models, and a private capability component that is not. We trace this geometry to concept-level structure: within a model, parent concepts are orthogonal to their child variation vectors; across models, concept displacements are parallel. Our theory falsifiably predicts (and experiments confirm) that fine-tuning preserves pointwise similarity but collapses displacement, and that relational distillation does the opposite.
  A major implication is that, because semantics align linearly but capabilities do not, capabilities can be imported from one model to another using a single cached forward pass through the source. We call this Shadow Casting. As a proof of concept, our SHADOWCLIP instantiation outperforms strong fine-tuned baselines at orders of magnitude less compute. A cache can be released alongside open model weights, letting one model's capabilities be downloaded and imported into any number of other models without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [363] [RRSI: Regularized Recursive Self-Improvement of Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24972)
*Peng Xia,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Yanfei Chen,Yufan Zhang,Yoonho Lee,Chengsong Huang,Han Yu,Zhongying CuiZhu,Yifei Ming,Huaxiu Yao,Burak Gokturk,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RRSI 通过正则化候选提议与选择，抑制智能体框架递归自进化中的过拟合，提升分布外泛化并降低 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体能力高度依赖其框架（提示、控制流、工具、记忆与上下文管理）。近期方法通过迭代提议与选择组件级编辑来自动化框架改进，形成智能体系统层面的递归自改进。但这种递归进化可能通过记忆训练任务而过拟合，表现为分布内增益显著、分布外却缩小甚至消失。

Method: 提出正则化递归自改进（RRSI），将正则化原则引入框架自改进：提议者采用时间退火预算，限制单个候选可捆绑的编辑数量，并基于进化历史鼓励未探索路径；选择者配备 critic 与 pruner，critic 过滤基准特定提议，pruner 移除过小、过贵或不再有用的改动。

Result: 在涵盖编码、智能体工作空间与工程设计任务的八个基准上，RRSI 在进化所用划分上最高提升 14.1 分，在五个分布外基准上最高提升 4.7 分，且所得框架比未正则化进化少用 30% 的策略 token。

Conclusion: 通过在提议与选择环节施加正则化约束，RRSI 偏好多可复用的智能体机制而非基准特定机制或噪声，有效缓解递归自改进的过拟合，兼顾分布内与分布外性能并提升 token 效率。

Abstract: An LLM agent's capability is largely magnified by its harness, namely the prompts, control flow, tooling, memory, and context management surrounding the frozen backbone model. Recent methods increasingly automate this process by iteratively proposing and selecting component-wise edits of an agent harness, practically establishing a form of recursive self-improvement (RSI) at the agent-system level. However, such recursive evolution may overfit by memorizing the training tasks, showing large in-distribution gains that shrink or even vanish on out-of-distribution benchmarks. We introduce Regularized Recursive Self-Improvement of Agent Harnesses (RRSI), which incorporates the principles of regularizations into harness self-improvement by constraining the evolution candidate proposal and selection. The proposer operates with a temporally annealed budget, limiting how many edits a candidate can bundle, and it encourages unexplored trajectories based on evolution history. The selector is equipped with a critic and a pruner: the critic screens benchmark-specific proposals, while the pruner, removes changes that are too small, too expensive, or no longer useful. Together these constraints favor reusable agent mechanisms over benchmark-specific ones or even noises. Across eight benchmarks spanning coding, agentic workspace and engineering design tasks, RRSI gains up to 14.1 points on the split it evolves against and up to 4.7 points on the five out-of-distribution benchmarks, while producing a harness that runs on 30% fewer policy tokens than the unregularized evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/google-research/rrsi and project page is https://regularized-rsi.com/.

</details>


### [364] [Reinforcement Learning Inspired Black-box Adversarial Attacks for Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24249)
*Florian Krone,Elena Hoemann,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RIBA——一种基于强化学习的黑盒对抗攻击方法，在Cifar10和ImageNet上以更少查询量超越现有攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络（卷积或Transformer）易受人类难以察觉的微小扰动影响，预测结果被显著改变，这对安全关键应用构成威胁。但多数攻击依赖白盒威胁模型，需要完全访问目标模型，实际不可行。因此需要更现实的黑盒攻击方法。

Method: 在更现实的黑盒威胁模型下，利用强化学习概念，针对不可微的目标模型优化扰动。由于强化学习算法已被优化得非常查询高效，成为设计黑盒对抗攻击的理想起点。据此提出强化学习启发的黑盒对抗攻击（RIBA）。

Result: 在Cifar10和ImageNet上对比多种模型与最先进攻击：对Cifar10的ResNet-18生成对抗图像所需中位查询数减少25.4%；对ImageNet的Vit-B/16模型减少22.5%。此外，RIBA在对抗训练模型上可匹敌白盒攻击的性能。

Conclusion: RIBA仅需少量查询即可高效生成对抗扰动，查询效率优于现有最先进攻击，且在对抗训练模型上达到白盒攻击水平，验证了强化学习在黑盒对抗攻击中的有效性。

Abstract: Neural networks, both convolution or transformer based, are essential for modern computer vision systems. However, they are vulnerable to small perturbations, almost imperceptible to humans, which significantly alter the model's prediction. These adversarial attacks are often considered to be a significant threat to the implementation of neural networks in safety-critical applications. Most attacks utilize the white-box threat model and therefore require full access to the target model, making them unrealistic to use in practice. We propose a novel approach under the more realistic black-box threat model that utilizes concepts from reinforcement learning to optimize perturbations with a non-differentiable target model. Reinforcement learning algorithms have already been optimized to be query efficient, making them an ideal starting point when designing black-box adversarial attacks. We show the success of our reinforcement learning inspired black-box adversarial attack (RIBA) in generating adversarial perturbations using only a small number of queries to the target model, by comparing it to state of the art attacks on different models on the Cifar10 and ImageNet data sets. RIBA takes $25.4\%$ fewer median queries to generate attacked images against a ResNet-18 on Cifar10 and $22.5\%$ fewer median queries to fool a Vit-B/16 model on ImageNet. Additionally, we demonstrate that RIBA can match the performance of white-box attacks on an adversarially trained model.

</details>


### [365] [Explainable Predictive Condition-based Maintenance of Naval-Propulsion Systems using Fuzzy Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24250)
*Dionisis Kalogeropoulos,Georgia Sovatzidi,Panagiotis G. Kalozoumis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合模糊决策树与深度残差神经网络的框架，用于船舶可解释性预测性维护，在公开数据集上达到99.24%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航运业对全球经济影响重大，需要有效的维护技术保障运营可用性与安全。预测性维护（PdM）相比传统维护方式具有损伤预测、减少停机、延长机械寿命、提升航行安全等优势。但现有PdM方法不向用户解释其结果，用户难以理解可能发生的故障，缺乏信任。

Method: 提出一种新颖框架，融合模糊决策树与深度残差神经网络，对海军舰船执行可解释的预测性维护。该框架能基于所用数据集生成模糊局部规则，并利用因果关系提供结果解释，使用户易于理解。

Result: 使用公开数据集进行实验，验证了所提框架的有效性，准确率达到99.24%。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持高预测精度的同时，为预测性维护结果提供可理解的因果解释，有助于提升用户信任，适用于船舶维护场景。

Abstract: The shipping industry has a significant impact on the global economy, emphasizing the need for operational availability and safety through the use of effective maintenance techniques. During the last decades, predictive maintenance (PdM) has emerged as a promising solution compared to the existing conventional maintenance systems. This is because it offers several advantageous functions, such as damage predictions for vessel components, reduced downtime, improved and extended life of machinery, as well as higher safety during voyages. However, existing methodologies developed for performing PdM do not provide explanations of their results to users, so that they can understand the failures that may occur. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a novel framework based on a fuzzy decision tree and a deep residual neural network, aiming to perform explainable PdM on naval vessels. The proposed framework is able to generate fuzzy local rules based on the dataset used, and can provide explanations of its outcomes, using cause-and-effect relationships, in a way that are understandable to users, thereby gaining their trust. Experiments using a publicly available dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, as it achieves an accuracy of 99.24%.

</details>


### [366] [High-Dimensional Online Change Point Detection with Adaptive Thresholding and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24278)
*Sven Jacob,Bardh Prenkaj,Weijia Shao,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于切片Wasserstein距离的变化点检测框架，通过一维化表示、Gamma分布阈值校准、自适应在线算法及对比解释，显著降低误报并提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变化点检测方法在高维场景下计算困难，且对检测到的变化缺乏解释，限制了实际应用。

Method: 利用切片Wasserstein距离将多元序列转化为一维分数；分析随机切片的分布并证明可用Gamma分布近似以校准阈值；提出结合自适应分位数阈值的在线检测算法；并设计生成对比解释的框架。

Result: 相比主流在线与离线基线，误报平均至少降低48%，同时保持竞争或更优的检测性能，并产生可解释的变化点标注。

Conclusion: 该框架兼顾可扩展性与可解释性，适合高风险场景部署。

Abstract: Change point detection (CPD) identifies abrupt and significant changes in sequential data, with applications in human activity recognition, financial markets, cybersecurity, manufacturing, and autonomous systems. Traditional CPD methods often face computational challenges in high-dimensional settings and typically provide limited explanations for detected changes, which can restrict their practical usability. This paper introduces a CPD framework that improves scalability and interpretability by leveraging the Sliced Wasserstein (SW) distance. Our contributions are fourfold: (1) we transform multivariate sequential data into one-dimensional scores using the SW distance, making the resulting representation compatible with existing CPD methods; (2) we analyze the distributional behavior of random slices of the SW distance and show that, under suitable assumptions, they can be approximated by a Gamma distribution, providing a principled basis for threshold calibration; (3) we propose a self-adapting online CPD algorithm that combines this SW-based score with an adaptive quantile-based threshold; (4) we introduce a model-specific framework for generating contrastive explanations for annotated change points. Empirically, our method reduces false positives by at least $48\%$ on average compared with popular online and offline CPD baselines, while maintaining competitive or superior detection performance. Code is available at https://github.com/jsve96/SWCPD_Code. At the same time, it produces interpretable change-point annotations, making it practical for deployment in high-stakes applications.

</details>


### [367] [SupportCal: Label-Free Calibration of Post-Trained LLMs via Reference Support and Corroboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24303)
*Linhan Luo,Lequan Lin,Dai Shi,Feng Chen,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Post-training可提升任务表现但会损害置信度校准，使PoLM比PLM更过度自信。由于任务标注校准数据昂贵，本文用对应PLM作为无标签参考进行事后校准。作者重新审视了此前的"一致门控"二元处理，诊断发现不一致样本的聚合效应呈非单调：适度纳入可改善校准，全量纳入则收益递减。提出SupportCal，对一致样本保留单位权重，对不一致样本按own-base PLM相对支持度和预训练参考的佐证赋予连续权重，并刻画加权目标存在有限最优温度的条件。在MedMCQA与MathQA上，SupportCal对几乎所有目标模型配置的ECE均低于仅一致基线，TweetEval Sentiment补充结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练虽提升任务性能，却使语言模型比其预训练版本更过度自信，破坏置信度校准。而获取任务特定的标注校准数据成本高甚至不可得，因此对应的预训练PLM成为天然的无标签校准参考。既有的一致门控方法直接排除不一致样本，因其会导致拟合温度过高、产生过度自信的反面（欠自信），作者认为这种二元处理值得重新审视。

Method: 首先进行受控的"重新引入不一致样本"诊断实验，发现非单调的聚合效应。基于此提出SupportCal：一种无标签事后校准方法，对一致样本保留单位权重；对不一致样本赋予连续权重，权重依据own-base PLM的相对支持度以及从规模兼容候选池中选出的预训练参考模型所提供的佐证来计算。此外，作者从理论上刻画了所得加权目标函数何时存在有限最优温度。

Result: 在MedMCQA和MathQA数据集上，SupportCal对几乎所有评估的目标模型配置都取得了比"仅一致"基线更低的ECE。在固定标签分类任务的TweetEval Sentiment上，补充实验显示出一致的模式。

Conclusion: 适度的不一致样本纳入是有益的，二元地排除不一致样本并非最优；通过基于PLM支持度的连续加权，可在无标签条件下有效改善后训练模型的置信度校准，且该加权目标在适当条件下存在有限最优温度。

Abstract: Post-training often improves task performance but can degrade confidence calibration, leaving post-trained language models (PoLMs) more overconfident than their corresponding pretrained language models (PLMs). Because task-specific labeled calibration data can be costly or unavailable, the corresponding pretrained PLM provides a natural label-free reference for post-hoc calibration. Prior agreement-gated PLM-referenced calibration fits a scalar temperature using only examples on which the PoLM and its PLM reference agree, excluding disagreement examples because direct alignment can drive the fitted temperature excessively high and induce under-confidence. We revisit this binary treatment. A controlled reintroduction diagnostic reveals a non-monotonic aggregate effect: admitting a moderate fraction of disagreement examples can improve calibration, whereas the benefit diminishes as unit-weight inclusion approaches the full disagreement set. We introduce SupportCal, a label-free post-hoc method that retains agreement examples at unit weight and assigns disagreement examples continuous weights based on the own-base PLM's relative support and corroboration from pretrained references selected from a size-compatible candidate pool. We further characterize when the resulting weighted objective admits a finite optimal temperature. Across MedMCQA and MathQA, SupportCal yields lower ECE than the agreement-only baseline for nearly all evaluated target-model configurations; supplementary TweetEval Sentiment results show the same pattern on a fixed-label classification task.

</details>


### [368] [A Distributional Optimisation Perspective on Combining Models in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24328)
*Congye Wang,Yan Lin,Zheyang Shen,Matthew A. Fisher,Chris. J. Oates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将集成与低秩适配器平均统一为熵正则化的分布优化问题，发现集成情形下目标函数凸、适配器平均下非凸，评估了包括泛函变分梯度下降在内的新旧算法，并在合成分类任务与大语言模型常识推理微调上进行了实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同模型的组合能提升机器学习任务性能，但通常单个模型的训练与组合规则是分开且凭经验选择的。分布优化（在概率分布集合上进行优化）的最新进展提供了原则性联合训练的机会，将模型集合视为待优化支撑点的离散分布，然而这类方法的潜力尚未被充分理解。

Method: 作者把两种标准组合策略——集成和低秩适配器平均——表述为熵正则化的分布优化；分析其目标函数的凸性；评估现有及新算法，包括变分梯度下降的泛函变体；并开展涵盖合成分类任务与大语言模型常识推理基准微调的实证研究。

Result: 目标是凸的（集成情形），但在适配器平均情形下非凸，因此现有的平均场朗之万动力学收敛保证仅能迁移到前者；论文同时对新旧算法进行了评估并给出了跨越合成任务与语言模型微调的实验发现。

Conclusion: 将模型组合视为熵正则化分布优化为联合训练提供了原则性框架；其凸性依赖于具体组合策略，收敛理论目前仅适用于集成情形，而算法设计与实证验证覆盖了从合成分类到大语言模型微调的多种场景。

Abstract: Combining predictions from different models can improve performance at machine learning tasks, but the training of the individual models and the rule used to combine them are typically chosen separately, and by ad hoc means. Recent advances in distributional optimisation (i.e. where the optimisation occurs over the set of probability distributions) offer an opportunity for principled joint training, viewing the collection of models as a discrete distribution whose support points are to be optimised, but the potential of these methods is not well-understood. In this paper we (1) cast two standard combination strategies - ensembles and low-rank adapter averaging - as entropy-regularised distributional optimisation, observing that the resulting objective is convex in the ensemble case but not in the adapter-averaging case, so that existing convergence guarantees for mean field Langevin dynamics transfer only to the former; (2) assess existing and novel algorithms for this task, including a functional variant of variational gradient descent; and (3) report an empirical study spanning synthetic classification tasks and fine-tuning of large language models on a commonsense reasoning benchmark.

</details>


### [369] [Pharmacokinetic State Space Models for Unbiased Prediction of Haemodynamic Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24338)
*Rithin Nagaraj,Sudiksha Chindula,Bhaskarjyoti Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出将药物输注史（丙泊酚与瑞芬太尼效应室浓度）纳入术中低血压（IOH）预测，采用 Mamba 架构实现高预测性能与恒定内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中低血压是全身麻醉常见且后果严重的并发症，但临床管理仍是被动反应式而非预测式。现有预测模型忽略了药物输注史这一与药理学直接相关的重要信号，作者认为药代动力学轨迹在平均动脉压（MAP）变化显现之前就已编码了即将发生血流动力学改变的信息。

Method: 模型在预测中引入药物输注史，特别是丙泊酚和瑞芬太尼的效应室浓度作为输入特征（通过消融实验验证其贡献）；同时采用带 lead-gap 过滤的训练策略以避免模型学习识别正在发生的低血压而非预测未来事件；并使用基于 Mamba 的架构处理序列，替代传统 Transformer。

Result: 模型达到 AUROC 0.7360、AUPRC 0.1794，相比随机猜测基线（AUPRC 0.0657）提升 2.73 倍；移除丙泊酚和瑞芬太尼效应室浓度后 AUPRC 相较完整模型下降 13.9%；不使用 lead-gap 过滤训练会使 AUROC 下降 16.7%；Mamba 架构在保持上述高性能的同时，在不同序列长度下内存占用恒定，而 vanilla Transformer 呈二次方显存增长。

Conclusion: 药代动力学轨迹（药物效应室浓度）确实携带了 MAP 显现前的血流动力学变化信号，是有效的预测特征；lead-gap 过滤对模型学习真正的预测而非同步检测至关重要；基于 Mamba 的架构兼具高预测性能与恒定内存占用，比 Transformer 更适合持续性术中部署。

Abstract: An Intraoperative Hypotension (IOH) event is a frequent complication during administration of general anaesthesia with serious downstream consequences, yet clinical management remains reactive and not predictive. Existing predictive models, however, ignore drug infusion history as a valuable signal for prediction despite its direct pharmacological relevance. Our model achieves an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.7360 and an Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC) of 0.1794, representing a 2.73-fold lift over the random guessing AUPRC baseline (0.0657), with the removal of propofol and remifentanil effect-site concentrations resulting in a 13.9% AUPRC drop compared to the full model. This is consistent with the hypothesis that pharmacokinetic trajectories encode impending haemodynamic changes before they manifest in the Mean Arterial Pressure (MAP). Additionally, this paper shows that training without lead-gap filtering degraded AUROC by 16.7%, empirically confirming that unfiltered models learn to detect ongoing hypotension rather than predict future events. Finally, a Mamba-based architecture achieves the aforementioned high prediction performance while maintaining a constant memory footprint across a range of sequence lengths, unlike the quadratic VRAM overhead typical of vanilla Transformers, making it the more practical choice for continuous intraoperative deployment.

</details>


### [370] [Explainable Neuro-Fuzzy Prediction for Trustworthy Decision-Making in Maritime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24358)
*Dionisis Kalogeropoulos,Georgia Sovatzidi,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释决策框架，将神经模糊预测模型与两阶段可解释组件结合，用于海事系统维护预测，兼具高精度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事系统需要提前预测维护需求，以避免危险和高昂代价。然而现有深度学习预测模型多为黑箱，缺乏可信度，因此需要兼顾预测性能与可解释性的方法。

Method: 框架包含神经模糊预测模型与两阶段可解释组件：第一阶段用基于梯度的显著图生成特征归因解释；第二阶段用模糊决策树提取局部规则。该框架通用，可嵌入任意深度学习方法，并以深度残差网络作为骨干。

Result: 在多个通用公开基准数据集上评估，并在海军推进系统数据集上验证早期故障检测效用。结果优于相关先进方法，平均AUC-ROC最高可达99%，同时具备可解释性优势。

Conclusion: 该框架是首个基于模糊逻辑、能同时提供特征级与局部规则级解释的黑箱模型框架，有助于通过用户可理解的机器推断提升决策可信度。

Abstract: Predicting when maritime systems require maintenance can be critical, avoiding hazards and costly consequences. To address this problem, this paper proposes an explainable decision-making framework that integrates a neuro-fuzzy prediction model with a two-stage explainable component. The first stage of this component produces feature-attribution explanations, using gradient-based saliency maps, and the second stage extracts local rules using a fuzzy decision tree. The proposed framework is generic and can be integrated into any deep learning-based approach, rendering it explainable. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first fuzzy logic-based framework enabling both feature-level and local rule-based explanations of black box models. This approach aims to foster trustworthiness in decision making through user-understandable machine inferences. The performance of the proposed framework using a deep residual-based neural backbone is evaluated on various general-purpose public benchmark datasets, and its utility in maritime is demonstrated in the context of early fault detection in a naval propulsion system dataset. The results indicate that it can provide predictions outperforming relevant state-of-the-art approaches, with an average AUC-ROC (Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve) value, reaching up to 99%, while offering the advantage of explainability.

</details>


### [371] [Prescriptive SVD-Inspired Attention via Spectral Energy Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24370)
*Vasileios Arampatzakis,Vasileios Sevetlidis,George Pavlidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“诊断—干预—验证”框架，验证SVDA注意力中频谱能量保留这一干预操作可在几乎无损精度下压缩注意力分数方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer的自注意力采用稠密点积，难以辨别内部哪些方向结构重要、哪些可被修改而不破坏模型；SVDA虽已让潜在注意力方向可被显式检查，但从“诊断解释”走向“可操作干预”的路径尚不明确。

Method: 提出诊断—干预—验证框架，并评估一种干预：在注意力分数通路中进行频谱能量保留。基于SVDA学习到的对角频谱，按ρ=0.90的保留准则移除部分分数方向，并在FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Food-101上进行测试。

Result: ρ=0.90准则移除24.5–53.7%的分数方向，参数减少2.6–4.3%，估计MACs减少2.8–5.4%；三个随机种子下，降维模型的平均精度变化为−0.03至+0.05个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持SVDA是一种内在可解释的注意力机制，其学习到的频谱提供了一个可操作坐标系，可对注意力分数形成进行确定性且可验证的修改。

Abstract: Self-attention is central to modern Transformer architectures, but its dense dot-product formulation makes it difficult to identify which internal directions are structurally important and which can be modified without disrupting the model. SVD-Inspired Attention (SVDA) addresses part of this problem by introducing a learned diagonal spectrum into the query-key score interaction, making latent attention directions explicitly inspectable through indicators such as spectral entropy, effective rank, sparsity, alignment, selectivity, and perturbation response. This paper examines the transition from diagnostic interpretation to operational intervention. A diagnosis--intervention--verification framework is proposed, and one intervention is evaluated: spectral energy retention in the attention-score pathway. Across FashionMNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Food-101, the $ρ=0.90$ prescription removes 24.5--53.7\% of score directions, reduces parameters by 2.6--4.3\%, and reduces estimated MACs by 2.8--5.4\%. The paired mean accuracy change of the dimension-reduced model ranges from $-0.03$ to $+0.05$ percentage points over three seeds. These results support SVDA as an intrinsically interpretable attention mechanism whose learned spectrum exposes an operational coordinate system for deterministic and verifiable modification of attention-score formation.

</details>


### [372] [Credit Access is Associated with Improved Food Security in the Horn of Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24382)
*Jordi Cerdà-Bautista,Vasileios Sitokonstantinou,José Manuel Veiga López-Peña,Duccio Piovani,José María Tárraga,Gustau Camps-Valls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用观察性机器学习框架估计信贷可得性与索马里及非洲之角急性粮食不安全之间的因果关联，发现信贷可得性提高与急性粮食不安全减少约2%相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化加剧威胁粮食安全，脆弱社区尤甚；在数据稀缺、受危机影响的地区，随机评估不可行，亟需替代性因果推断证据。

Method: 构建观察性机器学习框架，整合2015—2022年环境、社会经济与冲突相关异质数据，采用显式识别假设进行因果关联估计，并辅以稳健性与反驳检验。

Result: 信贷可得性提高与研究期内人群层面急性粮食不安全下降2%相关；鉴于平均16%人口处于危机状态，该效应在风险群体中具有实质意义。

Conclusion: 提供了金融可得性与粮食安全结果在数据稀缺、危机环境中关联的情境化证据，并为随机评估不可行时整合异质数据源提供了透明框架。

Abstract: The intensification of climate change poses a growing threat to food security, especially in vulnerable communities. This study employs an observational machine-learning framework to estimate the causal association between access to credit and acute food insecurity in Somalia and across the Horn of Africa, drawing on a harmonized dataset spanning key environmental, socioeconomic, and conflict-related factors from 2015 to 2022. Results indicate that greater credit access is associated with a 2% reduction in acute food insecurity at the population level over the study period. Given that, on average, 16% of the population is in crisis, this effect represents a meaningful shift within the at-risk group. We interpret these estimates under explicit identification assumptions and complement them with robustness and refutation tests. The results provide context-specific evidence on how financial access correlates with food security outcomes in data-scarce, crisis-affected settings, and offer a transparent framework for integrating heterogeneous data sources when randomized evaluations are infeasible.

</details>


### [373] [Machine Learning-Based Prediction of Childhood Stunting in Bangladesh: Fairness and Temporal Robustness Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24386)
*Md Ahshanul Haque,Muhammad Ashad Kabir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于2007—2022年孟加拉国人口与健康调查数据，开发机器学习模型预测0-59月龄儿童发育迟缓，评估时间稳健性与亚组公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童发育迟缓是孟加拉国重大公共卫生问题，反映儿童、母亲、家庭、社会经济与卫生服务等多因素导致的长期生长失败。研究旨在实现人群层面的发育迟缓预测，并检验模型在不同时间与亚组中的稳健性与公平性。

Method: 使用2007、2011、2014年三轮调查数据（18,844名儿童）建模，2018与2022年数据作为时间外部测试集；比较12种特征选择方法，最终采用KNN置换重要性筛选的预测变量集；评估11种模型，包括10种常规算法与一种预训练表格基础模型TabPFN；指标涵盖平衡准确率、AUROC、F1、Brier分数与期望校准误差；按儿童性别、居住地和社会经济地位考察亚组公平性。

Result: 最终样本18,844名儿童，迟缓率为35.05%。在开发集留出测试中，TabPFN平衡准确率最高（67.58%），常规模型中AdaBoost最高（67.51%）。时间测试中，BDHS 2018以Gradient Boosting表现最佳，BDHS 2022以XGBoost最佳。模型性能随调查轮次和亚组而变化。

Conclusion: 公共卫生预测建模中应重视时间验证、亚组公平性评估与模型解释的透明性；简单常规模型与基础模型性能接近，提示需结合时间与公平性维度综合选择模型。

Abstract: Childhood stunting remains a major public health concern in Bangladesh and reflects long-term growth failure influenced by child, maternal, household, socioeconomic, and health-service factors. This study used nationally representative Bangladesh Demographic and Health Survey data from 2007 to 2022 to develop machine learning models for population-level prediction of childhood stunting and to assess temporal robustness and subgroup fairness. Children aged 0-59 months with complete anthropometric and predictor data were included. Data from the 2007, 2011, and 2014 survey rounds were used for model development, while the 2018 and 2022 rounds were retained as temporal test datasets. Twelve feature-selection approaches were assessed, and the KNN permutation importance-selected predictor set was used for final model evaluation. Eleven machine learning models were evaluated: ten conventional algorithms and one pretrained tabular foundation model, TabPFN. Performance was assessed using balanced accuracy, AUROC, F1-score, Brier score, and expected calibration error. Subgroup fairness was examined by child sex, place of residence, and socioeconomic status. The final analytic sample included 18,844 children, of whom 35.05% were stunted. In the development hold-out test dataset, TabPFN showed the highest observed balanced accuracy overall at 67.58%, while AdaBoost showed the highest observed balanced accuracy among conventional models at 67.51%. In temporal testing, the highest observed balanced accuracy was found for Gradient Boosting in BDHS 2018 and XGBoost in BDHS 2022. Model performance varied across survey rounds and subgroups, highlighting the importance of temporal validation, subgroup fairness assessment, and transparent interpretation in public health prediction modeling.

</details>


### [374] [NAVIR: Neuromorphic Audio-Visual Speech Recognition for Robust Human-Robot Interaction on Edge Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24391)
*Leonidas Delimpasis,Panagiota Moraiti,Antonis Porichis,Panos Chatzakos,Michail Karamousadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NAVIR，首个面向BrainChip Akida神经形态处理器的端到端音视频语音识别系统，通过将时空编码分解为独立AkidaNet模块，在噪声环境下显著优于纯音频基线，并实现极低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中的声学噪声严重破坏纯音频语音识别。音视频语音识别（AVSR）可融合唇动线索与音频，但现有先进方案依赖三维卷积、循环单元和注意力模块，超出典型边缘设备的算力预算；而Akida类神经形态处理器仅原生支持顺序二维卷积推理。

Method: NAVIR将空间与时间编码分解为多个基于AkidaNet的独立模块：逐帧视觉编码器、时序视频编码器和频谱图音频编码器，由轻量预测头融合，并用受限束搜索解码。模型先在噪声增强音频上以CTC训练，再用量化感知训练微调。

Result: 在GRID基准上，量化音视频模型在噪声下未见说话人划分的WER为14.0%、重叠说话人划分为3.3%，而纯音频基线分别为22.5%和11.8%；工业命令语料上达98.6%命令准确率、1.5% WER。运算量分析显示脉冲形式相比ANN对应版本具13倍能效优势（平均放电率27.6%）。实测每推理能耗约为树莓派CPU的1/5、笔记本GPU的1/100以下，同时保持14.5次/秒推理速度。

Conclusion: 这是首个在此类神经形态硬件上运行的完整多模态AVSR流水线，验证了在边缘神经形态设备上实现抗噪、低功耗音视频语音识别的可行性。

Abstract: Voice-controlled interaction in industrial settings is hampered by acoustic noise, which severely degrades audio-only speech recognition. Audio-visual speech recognition (AVSR) addresses this by fusing lip-motion cues with the audio stream, but state-of-the-art pipelines rely on three-dimensional convolutions, recurrent units, and attention modules that exceed the budget of typical edge devices. We present NAVIR, an end-to-end AVSR system targeting the BrainChip Akida neuromorphic processor, which natively supports only sequential two-dimensional convolutional inference. The pipeline factorises spatial and temporal encoding into separate AkidaNet-based modules: a per-frame visual encoder, a temporal video encoder, and a spectrogram audio encoder, fused by a lightweight predictor head and decoded by constrained beam search. Models are trained with connectionist temporal classification on noise-augmented audio and then fine-tuned with quantization-aware training. On the GRID benchmark, the quantized audio-visual model reaches 14.0% word error rate (WER) under noise on the unseen-speaker split and 3.3% WER on the overlapped-speaker split, against 22.5% and 11.8% for audio-only baselines, and it attains 98.6% command accuracy at 1.5% WER on a task-specific industrial-command corpus. Operation-count analysis indicates a 13-fold energy advantage of the spiking formulation over its artificial neural network counterpart at 27.6% mean firing rate. On-board measurements show roughly 5-fold lower energy per inference than a Raspberry Pi central processing unit on the lip-reading model, and over 100-fold lower than a laptop graphics processing unit, while sustaining 14.5 inferences per second. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first complete multimodal AVSR pipeline running on neuromorphic hardware of this class.

</details>


### [375] [Climate Variability Modulates the Impact of Price Spikes on Food Insecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24394)
*Jordi Cerdà-Bautista,Vasileios Sitokonstantinou,Homer Durand,Gherardo Varando,Michele Ronco,Gustau Camps-Valls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"敏感性机制"（sensitivity regimes），按植被对ENSO响应的方向与强度对区域分层，并结合遥感、社会经济数据与因果机器学习，评估粮价飙升对撒哈拉以南非洲急性粮食不安全的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管气候变率决定市场扰动是否会升级为粮食危机，但像厄尔尼诺这样可提前数月追踪的大尺度气候模式，尚未被纳入粮食安全响应的早期预警体系。作者旨在填补这一空白，明确气候背景在粮食安全脆弱性中的关键作用，并推动前瞻性行动。

Method: 提出"敏感性机制"概念，即依据区域植被对厄尔尼诺南方涛动（ENSO）响应的方向和强度进行分层；整合遥感数据、社会经济数据与因果机器学习方法，估计粮价飙升对撒哈拉以南非洲急性粮食不安全的影响。

Result: 在ENSO系统性抑制植被的区域，粮价飙升使下个月处于急性风险的人口比例上升5.4个百分点；在植被不受ENSO影响或与其正相关的区域，估计效应较小（约2个百分点）且统计上不显著。结果表明气候背景对理解粮食安全脆弱性至关重要。

Conclusion: 敏感性机制可与业务化的粮价飙升触发条件结合以安排前瞻性行动：ENSO状态可提前数月标示脆弱区域，在这些区域对粮价飙升预先部署响应，能够避免急性粮食不安全的最大幅度上升。

Abstract: Climate variability influences whether a market disruption escalates into a food crisis, yet broad climate patterns like El Niño, tracked months before they alter hydro-climatic conditions, are still not incorporated as an early-warning component in food-security responses. We address this gap by introducing sensitivity regimes, a stratification of regions by the direction and strength of their vegetation response to the El Niño Southern Oscillation, and using them to estimate how food price spikes affect acute food insecurity across sub-Saharan Africa. Integrating remote sensing, socioeconomic data, and causal machine learning, we find that in regions where ENSO systematically suppresses vegetation, a price spike raises the share of the population at acute risk by 5.4 percentage points in the following month. In regions where vegetation is unaffected by or positively linked to ENSO, the estimated effect is smaller (around 2 percentage points) and statistically insignificant. These results demonstrate that climate context is critical for understanding food security vulnerabilities. Sensitivity regimes can be combined with operational price-spike triggers to stage anticipatory action: the ENSO state flags vulnerable regions months ahead, and a pre-positioned response in those regions to a price spike would avert the largest jump in acute food insecurity.

</details>


### [376] [Probabilistic Modelling of Operational Design Domains, A New Approach for Testing AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24397)
*Hans-Werner Wiesbrock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概率扩展本体（PEON），通过对本体附加概率分布并借助耦合与最优传输算法构建贝叶斯网络，实现代表性测试用例采样、严格的测试终止准则，以及测试结果再评估和训练数据平衡评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试流程应用于基于机器学习的系统（如车辆障碍物检测）时迅速失效：若测试中未检测到障碍物，无法进行经典的缺陷修复，AI系统的缺陷将始终存在，测试结果只能从统计角度解释，因此需要针对运行设计域（ODD）既完备又具代表性的测试集。

Method: 引入概率扩展本体（PEON）：在描述ODD的本体之上附加其划分上的概率分布。无需维护困难的条件概率表，只需指定边缘分布和函数式描述的依赖关系，再通过基于耦合和最优传输的算法补全为贝叶斯网络。

Result: 基于PEON可导出代表性测试用例的采样方法、针对给定质量目标和显著性水平的严格测试终止准则，以及用于重新评估既有测试结果和评估训练数据平衡性的方法；并以自动列车运行为例展示了复杂ODD的实际建模。

Conclusion: PEON为ML系统提供了兼顾完备性与代表性的统计测试框架，可支撑测试用例生成、终止判定及数据平衡评估，并在自动列车运行场景中得到验证。

Abstract: The conventional testing process quickly fails when applied to ML-based systems such as obstacle detection in vehicles: if an obstacle is not detected in a test, classical bug fixing is impossible and an AI system will always retain shortcomings. Test results can therefore only be interpreted statistically, which in turn requires test sets that are not only complete with respect to the operational design domain (ODD) of the system, but also representative of it. To this end, we introduce probabilistically extended ontologies (PEONs): ontologies describing the ODD, augmented with a probability distribution over the partitioning they induce. Instead of unmaintainable conditional probability tables, only marginal distributions and functionally described dependencies need to be specified; algorithms based on couplings and optimal transport complete this specification to a Bayesian network. From a PEON we derive the sampling of representative test cases, rigorous end-of-test criteria for given quality targets and significance levels, and methods for re-evaluating existing test results and for assessing the balance of training data. We demonstrate the practical modelling of a complex ODD using the example of automatic train operation.

</details>


### [377] [Prior-Amortized In-Context Bayesian Inference for Generalized Linear Mixed-Effects Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24422)
*Alex Kipnis,Marcel Binz,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 metabeta：预训练神经网络实现 GLMM 的先验可摊销上下文贝叶斯推断，测试时可接受不同先验族与超参数，零样本泛化；无需调参，比 NUTS 快 2–3 个数量级，且精度与 NUTS 相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层数据普遍，常用 GLMM 分析；贝叶斯推断虽提供校准的不确定性，但依赖 MCMC，NUTS 虽为金标准但速度慢，且每遇新数据集、模型或先验都需从头重启。已有神经后验估计器在训练时固定先验，缺乏灵活性。

Method: metabeta 是预训练神经网络，支持先验摊销的上下文贝叶斯推断。测试时可将先验族与超参数作为输入，实现零样本泛化。用两个 set transformer 与条件归一化流对应后验的两级结构（跨组共享的全局参数、每组的局部参数）。在覆盖连续、二值、计数结果的数百万模拟数据集上训练。默认用 Independence Metropolis-Hastings 针对未归一化后验精炼流后验，正确性依赖采样器而非网络；也可用流热启动 NUTS。

Result: 在含真值参数的受控基准上，metabeta 在参数恢复、校准与样本外预测方面与 NUTS 相当。在分布外真实数据集上，各类参数后验与 NUTS 高度一致，并在似然与先验误设、分布外预测变量、共线设计及数据稀缺情形下保持可靠。无需调参，推断速度比 NUTS 快 2–3 个数量级；热启动 NUTS 可获近乎相同推断并显著提升速度与稳定性。

Conclusion: metabeta 为 GLMM 提供快速、免调参、可靠的贝叶斯推断，可与 NUTS 精度媲美且具鲁棒性；模型开源、开放权重，可即刻部署。

Abstract: Hierarchical data is ubiquitous in the empirical sciences and is most commonly analyzed with generalized linear mixed-effects models (GLMMs). Bayesian inference for GLMMs yields calibrated uncertainty but requires MCMC; the No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS) is the gold standard but is slow and must restart from scratch for every new dataset, model and prior. We introduce metabeta, a pretrained neural network for prior-amortized in-context Bayesian inference over GLMMs. Unlike previous neural posterior estimators that fix the prior at training time, metabeta accepts prior families and hyperparameters as inputs at test time, enabling zero-shot generalization. Two set transformers and conditional normalizing flows mirror the posterior's two-level structure (global parameters shared across groups, local parameters per group). The model is trained on millions of realistic simulated datasets spanning continuous, binary, and count outcomes. By default, the flow posterior is refined by Independence Metropolis-Hastings against the unnormalized posterior, so its correctness rests on the sampler rather than the network; this yields tuning-free inference two to three orders of magnitude faster than NUTS. Alternatively, the flow can warm-start NUTS, giving nearly identical inference with substantially increased speed and stability. On controlled benchmarks with ground-truth parameters, metabeta matches NUTS in parameter recovery, calibration and out-of-sample prediction. On out-of-distribution real datasets, its posteriors closely match those of NUTS across all parameter types, and they remain faithful under misspecified likelihoods and priors, out-of-distribution predictors, collinear designs, and data-poor regimes. The model is open-source and open-weights and thus immediately deployable.

</details>


### [378] [1% of Tokens Can Be Enough: On Gradient Estimation in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24432)
*Huanxin Sheng,Zhiling Ye,Haonan Wang,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Jian Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏在线策略蒸馏(OPD)中教师监督的梯度估计问题，提出信息效率比(IER)用于token选择，在数学与医学推理任务上优于现有选择器，在0.1%-1%的稀疏预算下可媲美或超越完整OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏OPD仅对少量token施加教师监督，但即便是有用的教师指导，其梯度从采样得到的下一个token估计时会引入噪声。因此需要在分配稀疏监督时同时考虑"有用性"和"梯度估计的可靠性"。

Method: 在固定前缀处以信息几何视角研究该估计问题，基于信噪比分解提出信息效率比(IER)，刻画最优标量基线下的相对梯度估计误差；引入候选集近似，使基于IER的token选择成为可能，并可与现有有用性分数结合，同时保留采样的反向KL训练目标。

Result: 在数学与医学推理任务上，加入IER在多设置下改进了现有选择器；在0.1%-1%的小token预算下，稀疏配置可匹配或超过无token选择的完整OPD。

Conclusion: 在分配稀疏监督时，应同时考虑教师指导的有用性与梯度估计的可靠性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Sparse on-policy distillation (OPD) allocates teacher supervision to a small subset of tokens in student-generated trajectories. However, useful teacher guidance can yield a noisy update when its gradient is estimated from a sampled next token. We study this estimation problem at a fixed prefix in information geometry and propose an information-efficiency ratio (IER) based on a signal-to-noise decomposition. IER characterizes relative gradient estimation error under an optimal scalar baseline. A candidate-set approximation enables token selection based on IER and its combination with existing usefulness scores, while retaining the sampled reverse-KL training objective. On mathematical and medical reasoning tasks, adding IER improves existing selectors in multiple settings, with sparse configurations matching or exceeding full OPD without token selection at small token budgets of 0.1\%--1\%. These results support accounting for both usefulness and gradient-estimation reliability when allocating sparse supervision. Our code is available at https://github.com/BruceSheng1202/IER-OPD.

</details>


### [379] [Comparing Latent Concept Formation in State Space Models and Transformers via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24440)
*Rithin Nagaraj,Rupa Laalasa Oruganti,Prerna Subhashchandra Kunder,Ashwini M Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过稀疏自编码器在千万级token语料上对比Mamba-130m与Pythia-70m，发现99.98%的特征高度对齐，支持表征普遍性假说，分歧仅限于0.02%的刚性句法边缘情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba等选择性状态空间模型以固定大小的循环隐状态压缩历史上下文，这种信息瓶颈是否导致其与Transformer学习到根本不同的潜在表征，是机制可解释性的基础问题。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）对Mamba-130m和Pythia-70m在千万级token语料上进行大规模特征级对应分析，基于Jaccard分布度量特征对齐程度，并对少数分歧特征进行定性刻画。

Result: 未发现系统性的表征分歧：在观察到的Jaccard分布上，99.98%的Mamba特征聚集于对齐上界；仅有0.02%的特征发生分歧，且这些分歧与循环瓶颈限制刚性句法解析的假设一致——Pythia可对格式边缘情况做单义分解，而Mamba被迫将无关句法异常压缩进多义的"杂物抽屉"神经元。

Conclusion: 架构路由机制对核心语义理解的影响可能可忽略不计，表征分歧局限于极端结构性边缘，为普遍性假说提供了初步的特征级证据。

Abstract: The quadratic scaling of Transformer self-attention has driven the adoption of sub-quadratic Selective State Space Models (SSMs) like Mamba, which compress past context into a fixed-size recurrent hidden state. This strict informational bottleneck raises a foundational question for mechanistic interpretability: do SSMs and Transformers learn fundamentally distinct latent representations? In this work, we employ Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to conduct a large-scale, feature-level correspondence analysis between Mamba-130m and Pythia-70m over a 10-million token corpus. Contrary to hypotheses predicting widespread architectural divergence, we find no evidence of systematic representational divergence between architectures: across the observed Jaccard distribution, 99.98% of Mamba features cluster toward the upper alignment boundary, providing preliminary feature-level support for the Universality Hypothesis. We further identify and qualitatively characterize this microscopic fraction (0.02%) of diverging features, finding patterns consistent with the hypothesis that the recurrent bottleneck selectively limits the parsing of rigid syntax rather than broad semantic ontology. We demonstrate that while Pythia's unconstrained attention permits the monosemantic decomposition of distinct formatting edge-cases, Mamba is forced to compress unrelated syntactical anomalies into polysemantic "junk drawer" neurons to preserve state capacity. Collectively, these results suggest that architectural routing mechanisms may have negligible impact on core semantic understanding, with representational divergence confined to extreme structural margins.

</details>


### [380] [MUSE: Dependency-Aware Adaptation of a Frozen Vision Backbone for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24441)
*Xinying Cai,Junkai Lu,Yuhan Zhu,Xiaoyun Yu,Xiangfei Qiu,Jilin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MUSE，一个基于完全冻结MAE的依赖感知适配框架，通过VCR和TPR两个模块分别建模跨变量与跨周期/周期内依赖，并以可学习门控融合预测，在10个真实数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列预测应用广泛，近期大型视觉模型（LVM）通过迁移跨域视觉先验提供了新范式，但现有LVM方法面临两个挑战：一是难以平衡独立视觉表征空间与跨变量依赖建模，二是自然图像上预训练的视觉骨干难以适应时间序列图像的独特时序语义。

Method: 基于完全冻结的预训练MAE构建依赖感知适配框架MUSE。包含两个模块：变量上下文精炼模块（VCR）在每个变量内聚合共享时序信息并建模跨变量上下文依赖，同时保留独立视觉空间；时序-周期精炼模块（TPR）在不同编码器深度进行轻量级精炼，显式建模跨周期时序依赖与周期内周期性依赖。两模块独立产生预测，通过可学习的预测级门控进行融合。

Result: 在10个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，MUSE取得了最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: MUSE通过依赖感知的适配策略有效解决了LVM用于时间序列预测中的表征空间平衡和时序语义适配问题，验证了冻结视觉骨干加轻量精炼模块在多元时间序列预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series forecasting is essential to many real-world applications. Recent large vision models (LVMs) offer a promising paradigm by transferring cross-domain visual priors to time-series forecasting. However, existing LVM-based methods face two key challenges: balancing independent visual representation spaces with cross-variable dependency modeling, and adapting vision backbones pretrained on natural images to the distinct temporal semantics of time-series images. To address these challenges, we propose MUSE, a dependency-aware adaptation framework built on a fully frozen pretrained MAE. First, the Variable Context Refinement Module (VCR) aggregates shared temporal information within each variable and models cross-variable contextual dependencies while preserving independent visual spaces. Second, the Temporal-Periodic Refinement Module (TPR) performs lightweight refinement at different encoder depths and explicitly models across-period temporal dependencies and within-period periodic dependencies. The two modules independently produce forecasts, which are fused through a learnable prediction-level gate. Experiments on 10 real-world datasets demonstrate that MUSE achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [381] [RAILS: Retrieval-Augmented Incremental LLM Clustering at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24464)
*Armin Oliya,Aleksandra Sawczuk,Radosław Białobrzeski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAILS是一种检索增强的增量式LLM聚类器，将聚类转化为对不断增长的标签池的循环，并通过文档批处理和有界并发实现扩展，在六个公开基准上超越最强先前的LLM聚类方法，并已在生产SaaS工单主题发现流程中替代传统HDBSCAN阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产规模下使用LLM作为聚类器存在困难：提示无法容纳整个标签空间，逐文档串行处理无法提供实际工作负载所需的吞吐量。因此需要一种可扩展、支持增量操作的LLM聚类方案。

Method: 提出RAILS（检索增强的增量式LLM聚类器），将聚类转化为对不断增长的标签池的简单循环，并通过文档批处理与有界并发实现规模化。

Result: 在六个公开基准上，RAILS平均超越最强先前LLM聚类方法：准确率从51.2%提升至59.3%，NMI从67.2%提升至74.8%，ARI从45.4%提升至54.7%。在生产SaaS工单主题发现流程中，RAILS替代了传统HDBSCAN阶段，取得更高聚类质量、透明的提示驱动控制和有状态增量运行。

Conclusion: RAILS有效解决了LLM聚类在生产规模下的标签空间与吞吐量瓶颈，兼具更高的聚类质量和实用的生产部署价值。

Abstract: Using a Large Language Model (LLM) as the clusterer at production scale is hard: prompts cannot hold the entire label space, and per-document serial processing does not deliver the throughput real workloads require. We present RAILS, a retrieval-augmented incremental LLM clusterer that turns clustering into a simple loop over a growing label pool and scales through document batching with bounded concurrency. On six public benchmarks RAILS exceeds the strongest prior LLM-clustering method on average, lifting accuracy from 51.2% to 59.3%, NMI from 67.2% to 74.8%, and ARI from 45.4% to 54.7%. We further report production-deployment evidence from a SaaS ticket-topic-discovery pipeline, where RAILS has replaced a traditional HDBSCAN stage with higher clustering quality, transparent prompt-driven control, and stateful incremental operation.

</details>


### [382] [A Temporal Knowledge Graph for Music Festival Lineup Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24467)
*Julia Gastinger,Thilo Dieing,Christian Meilicke,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个覆盖55年、380个音乐节的时序知识图谱资源，将音乐节阵容预测形式化为时序链接预测任务，并评测了六种TKG预测模型及零样本LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐节阵容由艺术家、流派、发行、厂牌和历史演出等复杂关系共同决定，天然适合用时序知识图谱（TKG）预测来建模，但现有TKG基准缺乏具体真实应用场景的落地评测。

Method: 构建包含380个音乐节、55年跨度、超9万条演出四元组的TKG资源，附带音乐节、艺术家巡演与艺术家元数据；将阵容预测形式化为未来时间戳上艺术家与音乐节之间的时序链接预测，并评测六种TKG预测模型，同时与零样本大语言模型进行对比。

Result: 在六种TKG预测模型上完成了任务评测，分析了各模型的能力与局限，并与零样本LLM的表现进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该资源以具体真实应用领域为基础进行评测，补充了现有TKG基准，为TKG预测评估提供了新的应用导向数据集。

Abstract: Music festival lineups emerge from complex relationships among artists, genres, releases, labels, and past performances, making the prediction of future lineups a natural fit for temporal knowledge graph (TKG) forecasting. In this work, we present a TKG covering 380 festivals over 55 years, comprising more than 90K festival performance quadruples along with information on festivals, artist tours, and artist metadata, and release it as a resource for TKG forecasting evaluation. We formalize festival lineup forecasting as temporal link prediction between artists and festivals at future timestamps.
